Motivation: 探索超引力在Carroll极限（光速趋近于零的非相对论极限）下的行为，以理解该极限下超对称理论的结构与性质。

Method: 采用电性和磁性两种Carroll极限方案，对N=1超引力在四维时空中的场方程和对称性进行显式推导。

Result: 成功得到了N=1超引力在两种Carroll极限下的具体形式，并指出该推导方法适用于更一般的扩展超引力理论。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究Carroll极限下超对称引力理论提供了系统框架，且具有扩展到更高超对称性模型的潜力。

Abstract: The electric and magnetic carrollian limits of $N=1$ supergravity in $D=4$ spacetime dimensions are explicitly derived. The approach is general and applies also to extended supergravity models

</details>


### [103] [Gauge-Invariant Off-Shell Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08426)
*Kang-Sin Choi,Hyeseon Im*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在量子场论中定义了一个规范不变且可重整化的离壳质量函数。通过将 pinch 技术推广到任意长的费米子线，构建了规范不变且与过程无关的自能和顶点函数。Ward-Takahashi 恒等式在每一点局域作用，精确抵消了规范依赖部分，得到 Feynman 规范下的自能。在壳重整化后得到的质量函数是规范不变、方案无关的，且在极点处等于物理质量；该自能还产生了一个红外有限、可直接与 Schwinger-Dyson 方程和格点结果比较的标量质量函数。抵消在内部费米子线上是局域的，且完全离壳成立，因此自能在具有任意多外光子的振幅的每个内部段上都有良好定义；通过计算最小的离壳 Compton 振幅明确验证了这一点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子场论中，自能是规范依赖的，导致离壳质量函数缺乏规范不变性，难以与实验或非微扰方法（如 Schwinger-Dyson 方程、格点 QCD）直接比较。论文旨在构建一个规范不变、过程无关且可重整化的离壳质量函数。

Method: 将 pinch 技术推广到任意长费米子线，通过局域 Ward-Takahashi 恒等式在每个顶点处抵消规范依赖部分，将规范依赖的自能变换为 Feynman 规范自能，从而得到规范不变的自能和顶点函数。

Result: 得到了规范不变、方案无关的离壳质量函数，其在壳极点等于物理质量；导出了红外有限的标量质量函数，可与 Schwinger-Dyson 和格点结果直接比较；通过最小离壳 Compton 振幅计算验证了该构造在具有任意多外光子的振幅上的普适性。

Conclusion: 该方法为量子场论中规范不变的离壳质量函数提供了严格定义，弥合了微扰论与非微扰方法之间的鸿沟，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We define a gauge-invariant and renormalized off-shell mass function in quantum field theory. The conventional self-energy is gauge-dependent off-shell. We generalize the self-energy, together with the corresponding vertex function, to be gauge-invariant and process-independent. To do this, we extend the pinch technique to an arbitrarily long fermion line. The Ward-Takahashi identity acting locally at each vertex cancels the gauge-dependent piece exactly, leaving the Feynman-gauge self-energy. On-shell renormalization then yields a mass function that is gauge-invariant, scheme-independent and equal to the physical mass at the pole; the same self-energy also yields an infrared-finite scalar mass function directly comparable with Schwinger-Dyson and lattice determinations. The cancellation is local to the internal fermion line and holds entirely off-shell, so the self-energy is well-defined on each internal segment of an amplitude with arbitrarily many external photons; we demonstrate this explicitly by computing the minimal off-shell Compton amplitude.

</details>


### [104] [Gram--Wishart--Stiefel formulation of the $N=2$, large--$d$ gauge theory in 1D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08481)
*Badis Ydri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了BFSS/BMN矩阵量子力学晶格端点理论的Gram/Wishart/Stiefel形式化，通过将端点自由度重组为秩二Wishart特征值和相对Stiefel角变量，分析了holonomy不变量，并解决了大d微扰论中截断伪影问题，恢复了具有物理移位质量的Wishart鞍点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将先前得到的BFSS/BMN矩阵量子力学晶格端点理论用Gram/Wishart/Stiefel变量重新表述，以便更直接地分析holonomy不变量A、B和R²，并解决大d极限下微扰论截断导致的表观边界与连续极限不相容的问题。

Method: 采用Gram/Wishart/Stiefel形式化，将端点自由度重组为秩二Wishart特征值和相对Stiefel角变量；在大R对齐渐近下，将holonomy势的通用线性贡献-A吸收到高斯扇区中，产生移位质量参数(α_Λ)_eff=α_Λ-1/2；利用纯-A理论（Cartesian变量下精确可解）固定领先Bessel/HCIZ结构；对横向B型展开及其非多项式toy完备化求和，并平衡补偿性+A项。

Result: 有限多项式截断导致表观的大d微扰论边界，但该边界被证明是截断伪影；在求和局部横向完备化并平衡补偿性+A项后，恢复了具有物理移位质量的Wishart鞍点；连续行为复现了D_Λ-道的普适-2d贡献，而各向异性的β_Λ-道超出了纯横向B型描述的范畴。

Conclusion: 本文建立的Gram/Wishart/Stiefel形式化为理解BFSS/BMN矩阵量子力学的晶格端点理论提供了新的视角，解决了微扰论截断伪影问题，并明确了该形式化在描述不同通道（D_Λ-道普适、β_Λ-道超出范畴）时的适用范围。

Abstract: We develop in this paper the Gram/Wishart/Stiefel formulation of the \(N=2\), large--\(d\) planar endpoint theory of the BFSS/BMN matrix quantum mechanics on the lattice, obtained in our previous work. In this formulation, the endpoint degrees of freedom are reorganized into rank--two Wishart eigenvalues and relative Stiefel angular variables. This allows the holonomy invariants \(A\), \(B\), and \(R^2=A^2+B^2\) to be analyzed directly in terms of radial and angular Gram data.
  A central point is the large-\(R\) aligned asymptotics of the holonomy potential. Its universal linear contribution \(-A\) is absorbed into the Gaussian sector, producing the shifted mass parameter
  \((α_Λ)_{\rm eff}=α_Λ-1/2\). In the Gram/Wishart/Stiefel variables, the exact \(O(2)\) angular integral encodes this shifted sector in a rank--two Bessel kernel. The pure \(-A\) theory, which is exactly solvable in Cartesian variables, then fixes the leading Bessel/HCIZ structure: its exponential part selects the aligned configuration, while its prefactor removes the spurious doubled Wishart entropy.
  We then apply this structure to the transverse \(B\)-type expansion and its non-polynomial toy completion. Finite polynomial truncations lead to an apparent large--\(d\) perturbativity bound incompatible with the continuum limit, but this bound is shown to be an artifact of truncation. After summing the local transverse completion and balancing the compensating \(+A\) term, the Wishart saddle is recovered with the physical shifted mass. The resulting continuum behavior reproduces the universal \(-2d\) contribution of the \(D_Λ\)-channel, while the genuinely anisotropic \(β_Λ\)-channel lies outside the scope of a pure transverse \(B\)-type description.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum corrections to the near-extremal thermodynamics of (warped) BTZ black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08482)
*Emilie Despontin,Stéphane Detournay,Robinson Mancilla,Chiara Toldo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究了BTZ和扭曲BTZ黑洞在近极端热力学中的单圈效应，重点关注极端喉道中变为零模的本征模的演化。分析基于三维拓扑质量引力理论，利用Newman-Penrose形式，发现旋转模对近极端处理至关重要，不能在不指定边界条件的情况下忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解BTZ和扭曲BTZ黑洞在近极端条件下的热力学行为，特别是极端喉道中零模的演化及其导致的Schwarzian和旋转扇区的热效应。

Method: 使用三维拓扑质量引力理论（含引力Chern-Simons项）和Newman-Penrose形式，对BTZ黑洞进行近视界计算与全几何本征值问题对比，并将相同策略推广到扭曲BTZ情形。

Result: 识别了BTZ黑洞在低温下的Schwarzian扇区和旋转扇区如何提升；发现旋转模对BTZ和扭曲BTZ黑洞的近极端处理均不可或缺，其取舍依赖于边界条件。

Conclusion: 旋转模在近极端黑洞热力学中具有关键作用，不能随意丢弃，必须在明确边界条件的前提下进行一致处理。

Abstract: We study one-loop effects in the near-extremal thermodynamics of BTZ and warped BTZ black holes, with particular emphasis on the fate of eigenmodes that become zero modes in the extremal throat. Our analysis is formulated in three-dimensional Topologically Massive Gravity, a higher derivative theory characterized by the presence of a gravitational Chern--Simons term, and it makes use of the Newman--Penrose formulation. For BTZ, we compare the near-horizon computation with the full-geometry eigenvalue problem and identify how the Schwarzian and rotational sectors are lifted at small temperature. We then extend the same strategy to warped BTZ. We find that rotational modes are essential for a consistent near-extremal treatment of both BTZ and warped BTZ black holes, and cannot be discarded without first specifying the boundary conditions.

</details>


### [106] [The Remnant of an Evaporating Rotating Regular Black Hole from the Generalized Entropy in the Final Stage of Evaporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08661)
*Shingo Takeuchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过将旋转正则黑洞的质量表示为$m_{\rm ext}+α$，基于广义熵非负性及熵中面积项与修正项符号不变的假设，确定了修正项消失的下界$α_1$，表明黑洞质量不能小于$m_{\rm ext}+α_1$，即在蒸发末期产生残留体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旋转正则黑洞在蒸发末期的行为，特别是通过广义熵的非负性约束来探讨残留体的存在可能性。

Method: 将黑洞质量参数化为$m_{\rm ext}+α$，利用广义熵非负性及熵中面积项与修正项符号在整个蒸发过程中不变的假设，求解修正项为零时的$α$下界。

Result: 修正项消失时对应的$α$值$α_1$是有限的，表明黑洞质量下限为$m_{\rm ext}+α_1$，即蒸发末期形成残留体。

Conclusion: 旋转正则黑洞在蒸发末期存在残留体，其质量下限由广义熵的非负性条件决定；正则化处理考虑了中心区域精细结构在蒸发末期的相关性，旋转假设增加了结论的普遍性。

Abstract: We express the generalized entropy (GE) of the Hawking radiation in the final stage of an evaporating rotating regular black hole (BH) by writing the mass of the BH as $m_{\rm ext}+α$, where $m_{\rm ext}$ represents the mass at the extremal limit and $α$ is a parameter. Generally, entropy is non-negative. Based on this fact, we assume that, in the GE considered in this study, the signs of the contributions from the area term and the correction term remain unchanged throughout the entire evaporation process of the BH. Therefore, we regard $α$ at which the correction term vanishes as its lower bound and determine it. As a result, we find that such a value of $α$ is finite. Denoting this value by $α_1$, this result indicates that the mass of the BH cannot become smaller than $m_{\rm ext}+α_1$, which can be interpreted as the emergence of a remnant at the final stage of BH evaporation. The BH considered in this study is a rotating regular BH. The regularization is motivated by the fact that the fine structure of the central region becomes relevant in the final stage of evaporation. A rotating BH is considered from the viewpoint of generality.

</details>


### [107] [Entanglement Wedge Reconstruction without Holographic Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08684)
*Seiji Terashima*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文质疑了全息量子纠错（HQECC）作为纠缠楔重构的普遍解释，认为在有限N的全息CFT中不存在区域无关的逻辑体算符，纠缠楔重构应理解为“子区域互补性”而非共享逻辑算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清纠缠楔重构与全息量子纠错解释之间的关系，指出后者要求存在区域无关的逻辑体算符，而这种算符在有限N的全息CFT中并不存在。

Method: 通过局域性论证展示共同重构必须与互补区域的保编码局域代数对易；分析HaPPY型码的机制与普通全息CFT的区别；论证应力张量等低能可观测量的物理支撑和引力穿戴效应。

Result: 有限N的全息CFT中不存在全息量子纠错；全局与子区域HKLL公式在N=∞时的一致性是自由理论的结果；真正的纠缠楔重构应理解为子区域互补性。

Conclusion: 纠缠楔重构不应等同于全息量子纠错，每个边界区域有其自身的保编码低能代数和区域适配的体解释，而非共享的逻辑算符。

Abstract: Bulk reconstruction is a central problem in AdS/CFT, and entanglement wedge reconstruction is its subregion version. We argue that this subregion statement should be separated from the stronger holographic quantum error correction interpretation, in which one region-independent logical bulk operator has code-preserving representatives in several boundary regions. A simple locality argument shows that such a common reconstruction must commute with the code-preserving local algebras in the complementary regions. This is the mechanism realized in HaPPY-type codes: the erased regions are blind to a protected logical algebra. An ordinary finite $N$ holographic CFT does not have such a protected invisible sector for supergravity fields. Its low-energy local observables, in particular, suitably smeared stress tensors, detect the physical support and gravitational dressing of ordinary bulk operators, up to possible center or superselection data. Thus, there is no such holographic quantum error correction and the $N=\infty$ agreement of global and subregion HKLL formulae is a free-theory statement. What remains is entanglement wedge reconstruction without holographic quantum error correction, or subregion complementarity: each boundary region has its own code-preserving low-energy algebra and its own region-adapted bulk interpretation, rather than a shared logical operator.

</details>


### [108] [The mini-Page Curve in Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08737)
*Ricardo Espíndola,Shoichiro Miyashita*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过在二维流形几何中构建从AdS₂边界到dS₂静态区域的平滑插值模型，利用交叉积构造将centaur代数提升为Type II_∞因子，计算了热场双体参考态与霍金对态之间的熵差，发现该熵差呈现特征性的mini-Page曲线，其最小值出现在τ≈β/8处，标志着量子信息开始逃逸宇宙学视界的时间；同时证明代数熵与追踪发射粒子沿视界的纠缠楔割的广义熵一致，并通过相对模流得到Lyapunov指数λ=2π/β，识别了置乱时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞信息悖论促使人们广泛研究信息如何及何时从蒸发的黑洞中逃逸，本文旨在回答宇宙学视界的类似问题：单个霍金对何时开始将信息携带出德西特视界。

Method: 在一类二维流形几何中构建从渐近AdS₂边界到dS₂静态区域的平滑插值模型；通过由局域算符及其模共轭构建的探针态模拟霍金对发射；利用交叉积构造将centaur可观测代数提升为Type II_∞因子；计算热场双体参考态与霍金对态之间的熵差；将分析扩展到微正则系综并研究相对模流。

Result: 熵差呈现特征性的mini-Page曲线：从接近零开始，在τ≈β/8处达到最小值后再次增加；代数熵与追踪发射粒子沿视界的纠缠楔割的广义熵一致；相对模流产生的Lyapunov指数为λ=2π/β。

Conclusion: 最小值位置τ≈β/8被解释为量子信息开始逃逸宇宙学视界的时间；Lyapunov指数λ=2π/β将置乱时间识别为该对携带的信息能被静态区域观测者获取的特征时间尺度。

Abstract: The black hole information paradox has motivated extensive study of how and when information escapes from evaporating black holes. Here we address the analogous question for cosmological horizons: when does an individual Hawking pair begin to carry information out of a de Sitter horizon? We study this problem in a class of two-dimensional flow geometries that interpolate smoothly between an asymptotic AdS$ _2 $ boundary and a dS$ _2 $ static patch. Modeling the emission of a Hawking pair via a probe state constructed from local operators and their modular conjugates, we promote the centaur algebra of observables to a Type II$ _\infty $ factor through the crossed-product construction. This allows us to compute the entropy difference between the thermofield-double reference state and the Hawking-pair state. We find that this difference traces a characteristic mini-Page curve for the cosmological horizon: it starts near zero, reaches a minimum near $ τ\approx β/8 $ before increasing again. We interpret the location of the minimum as the time at which quantum information begins to escape the cosmological horizon. Extending the analysis to the microcanonical ensemble, we show that the algebraic entropy coincides with the generalized entropy of an entanglement wedge cut that tracks the emitted particle along the horizon. Furthermore, the relative modular flow generated between the two states yields a Lyapunov exponent $λ=2π/β$, identifying the scrambling time as the scale at which the information carried by the pair becomes accessible to a static-patch observer.

</details>


### [109] [Decoupling Limit of Quiver Theories and the Angular Spectra of Extreme C-metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08747)
*Peng Yang,Kilar Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端带电C度规的角特征值问题，通过双四维N=2超对称规范理论中的退耦极限，推导了参数字典和重整化Matone关系，并利用瞬子计数方法建立了代数量子化条件，得到了与数值结果一致的角特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端带电C度规的角特征值问题在极端极限下（Q→M）控制微分方程从具有五个正则奇点的Fuchs型方程退化为汇合扩展Heun方程，需要新的分析方法来求解其角谱。

Method: 在双四维N=2 SU(2)×SU(2)线性箭形规范理论中构建退耦极限，推导参数字典和重整化Matone关系以吸收奇点融合引起的宏观留数偏移；基于角方程的规则边界条件，采用瞬子计数方法建立代数量子化条件。

Result: 成功得到了与数值结果一致的角特征值。

Conclusion: 通过规范理论/重力对偶方法有效解决了极端带电C度规的角特征值问题，验证了该分析方法的有效性。

Abstract: We investigate the angular eigenvalue problem of the extreme charged C-metric. In the extreme limit ($Q \to M$), the governing differential equation degenerates from a Fuchsian equation with five regular singular points into a Confluent Extended Heun Equation. To evaluate the angular spectrum analytically, we formulate a decoupling limit within the dual four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$, $\mathrm{SU(2)}\times \mathrm{SU(2)}$ linear quiver gauge theory. Within this framework, we derive the parameter dictionary and renormalized Matone relations, which absorb the macroscopic residue shifts induced by the singularity fusion. Based on the regular boundary conditions of the angular equation, we utilize the instanton counting method to establish an algebraic quantization condition, yielding angular eigenvalues consistent with numerical results.

</details>


### [110] [Entropy bounds, Geroch process, and the sign of deformation parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04905)
*Bijan Bagchi,Akshat Pandey,Parmest Roy,Sauvik Sen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于Geroch过程，在广义不确定性原理（GUP）框架下研究了黑洞形变对贝肯斯坦熵界的影响，发现负GUP形变使熵界放松，正形变则使其收紧，可解释为普朗克尺度下近邻红移的修正响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在广义不确定性原理（GUP）背景下，黑洞形变如何影响贝肯斯坦熵界，从而检验普朗克尺度量子引力效应对热力学第二定律的修正。

Method: 采用Geroch提出的从视界附近将系统投入黑洞的过程，在(3+1)维和(2+1)维时空中，基于半经典处理分析GUP形变参数对熵界的影响。

Result: 负GUP形变参数导致熵界普遍放松，正GUP形变参数则使熵界收紧；该效应在两种维度下均成立。

Conclusion: GUP对熵界的修正可解释为普朗克尺度下近邻红移的量子修正响应，为量子引力效应在黑洞热力学中的体现提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Based on Geroch's process of dropping a system into a black hole from the vicinity of the horizon, we investigate in this paper the influence of deformation on the Bekenstein entropy bound both for (3+1) and (2+1) dimensions in the context of a generalized uncertainty principle (GUP). While providing a coherent framework that sets an upper limit on the entropy across dimensions we show, within a semiclassical treatment, that while a negative GUP deformation yields a universal relaxation of the bound, a positive deformation tightens it. Our results may be interpreted as a response to Planck-scale modifications of the near-horizon redshift.

</details>

### [111] [Factorization of elastic, single, and double diffractive $pp$ scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07788)
*Philipp B. Aretz,Kyle Lee,Stella T. Schindler,Iain W. Stewart*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用有效场论技术，在Regge极限下对弹性、单衍射和双衍射前向质子-质子散射进行因子化分析，发现衍射部分子分布函数在ep和pp衍射中不具有普适性，但快度反常维度是普适的，且色单态（坡密子）演化方程可在振幅层面确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释在Regge极限下弹性、单衍射和双衍射前向pp散射中，为何ep衍射中出现的衍射PDF在pp衍射中不作为普适的强子函数出现，并探究ep与pp衍射之间强子函数的普适性与非普适性。

Method: 采用有效场论技术，在Regge极限（|t|≪s）下对涉及大快度间隔的pp散射过程进行因子化分析，特别针对|t|∼Λ_QCD²和一般情况讨论强子函数的普适性，并分析快度反常维度和色单态演化方程。

Result: 1) 衍射PDF在ep和pp衍射中不具普适性；2) 当|t|∼Λ_QCD²时，ep和pp衍射的强子函数不同，即非普适；3) 快度反常维度在ep和pp衍射过程中是普适的；4) 色单态（坡密子）演化方程可在振幅层面确定。

Conclusion: ep和pp衍射中的强子函数不具有普适性，但快度反常维度具有普适性，且色单态演化方程可在振幅层面统一描述。

Abstract: We use effective field theory techniques to factorize elastic, single, and double diffractive forward $pp$ scattering in the Regge limit $|t|\ll s$, where $t$ is the squared momentum transfer. These processes involve a large rapidity gap and comprise about half the total $pp$ cross section. We explain why the diffractive PDFs appearing in $ep$ diffraction do not appear as universal hadronic functions for $pp$ diffraction. For $|t|\sim Λ_{\rm QCD}^2$, we show that the hadronic functions in $ep$ and $pp$ diffraction differ, and hence are non-universal. In general, we prove that rapidity anomalous dimensions are universal between diffractive $ep$ and $pp$ processes, and that color-singlet (Pomeron) evolution equations can be determined at the amplitude level.

</details>


### [112] [PDF effects in high-mass Drell-Yan SMEFT analyses across flavour space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07791)
*David Marzocca,Manuel Morales-Alvarado*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了高质量Drell-Yan双轻子产生过程中，部分子分布函数（PDF）轮廓分析对标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）约束的影响，发现PDF不确定性与SMEFT Wilson系数之间存在强烈的味依赖相关性，其中第一代夸克算符受PDF轮廓影响最大，而角分布信息能有效缓解简并性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量Drell-Yan过程对SMEFT半轻子四费米子算符极为敏感，但大x区域的PDF不确定性可能掩盖或模仿新物理信号。现有研究未充分评估PDF轮廓效应在不同夸克味结构下的差异，因此需要系统研究PDF轮廓对SMEFT约束的味依赖影响。

Method: 通过对SMEFT Wilson系数和PDF nuisance参数进行联合拟合，分别采用当前数据和HL-LHC投影数据，研究不同夸克味算符下PDF轮廓对SMEFT灵敏度的退化程度，并利用角分布信息分析简并性缓解效果。

Result: PDF轮廓导致SMEFT灵敏度出现强味依赖退化：第一代夸克算符受最大影响，其效应与高x价夸克亮度高度相关；重味夸克算符受影响较小但存在非可忽略的算符依赖变化。PDF轮廓同时反作用于PDF扇区。角分布信息能有效减少SMEFT方向之间以及SMEFT效应与PDF形变之间的简并性。

Conclusion: PDF不确定性在高能SMEFT拟合中的重要性并非均匀分布，必须根据味空间进行味依赖评估；角分布信息是缓解简并性的重要手段。

Abstract: High-mass Drell-Yan dilepton production provides one of the most sensitive probes of semileptonic four-fermion operators in the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), thanks to the energy growth of the corresponding contributions. At the same time, these measurements also constrain parton distribution functions (PDFs) in the large-$x$ region, where PDF uncertainties can mimic or obscure smooth new-physics effects. In this work we study how the impact of PDF profiling on SMEFT constraints depends on the quark-flavour structure of the effective operators, by performing a joint fit of SMEFT Wilson coefficients and PDF nuisance parameters. We find that PDF profiling induces a strongly flavour-dependent degradation of the SMEFT sensitivity, both with current data and in HL-LHC projections. The largest broadenings occur for operators involving first-generation quarks, whose effects are correlated with the high-$x$ valence-quark luminosities that dominate the high-mass spectrum. Operators involving heavier quark flavours are less affected, although with non-negligible operator-dependent variations. As a byproduct of our analysis, we obtain an estimate on the changes induced in the PDF sector by profiling SMEFT effects. We also show that angular information provides an important handle to reduce degeneracies among SMEFT directions and between SMEFT effects and allowed PDF deformations. These results demonstrate that the relevance of PDF uncertainties in high-energy SMEFT fits is not uniform across flavour space, and must be assessed in a flavour-dependent way.

</details>


### [113] [Dissecting Parton Showers with Multi-Point Energy Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07792)
*Mark Gonzalez,Philip Harris,Kyle Lee,Ian Moult,Simon Rothman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探索了喷注中四点能量关联函数的现象学，识别了实验上可行的投影方案，通过Herwig和Pythia的详细现象学研究，发现自旋关联在实验可及区域中影响较弱，强烈激励完整的QCD四点关联函数计算以检验部分子簇射结果，为LHC实验测量和下一代部分子簇射探针奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量关联函数的高度微分性质使其成为研究喷注内多共线因子化和方位角结构的理想探针，而四点关联函数的现象学尚未被充分探索。

Method: 识别可实验实现的投影方案，利用Herwig和Pythia进行部分子簇射模拟，并与运动学极限下的解析计算进行对比，以区分自旋关联和运动学方位角关联。

Result: 在实验可及的动力学区域，自旋关联效应是次主导的；提供了可用于实验的参数化和分析算法。

Conclusion: 本研究为LHC上四点能量关联函数的实验测量及作为下一代部分子簇射探针的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: The last several years have seen tremendous progress in the ability to both compute and measure multi-point correlations in energy flux. The highly differential nature of energy correlators makes them ideal probes of multi-collinear factorization and azimuthal structure within jets. In this paper, we explore the phenomenology of four-point correlators in jet substructure. We identify experimentally realizable projections that probe different factorization channels onto splitting tensors and splitting functions. We perform a detailed phenomenological study using both Herwig and Pythia. By comparing parton shower results with analytic calculations in kinematic limits, we are able to disentangle intrinsic spin correlations from kinematic azimuthal correlations. In experimentally accessible kinematic regions, we find the spin correlations are subdominant, strongly motivating a complete calculation of the four-point correlator in QCD to provide a test of the parton shower results. We also present parameterizations and analysis algorithms that can be used experimentally. Our work sets the stage for the experimental measurement of these observables at the LHC, and their use as probes of the next generation of parton showers.

</details>


### [114] [Flavor Hierarchies the Right Way](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07794)
*Pavel Fileviez Perez,Clara Murgui*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于universal seesaw机制的费米子质量生成框架，通过扩展阿贝尔规范对称性、引入矢量样费米子和标量场，在禁止普通汤川耦合的同时允许顶夸克耦合，从而产生带电费米子质量层级、轻中微子质量以及CP破坏，并保持QCD真空角为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释标准模型中费米子质量层级、中微子质量以及CP破坏参数的起源，同时解决强CP问题。

Method: 扩展标准模型，引入作用于右手费米子的阿贝尔规范对称性、矢量样费米子和标量场。禁止普通汤川耦合（除顶夸克外），通过费米子与矢量样扇区的混合产生质量层级；中微子通过Majorana seesaw获得质量；CP在紫外精确，通过标量真空期望值自发破缺，产生的CP破坏相位通过universal seesaw块结构实现Nelson-Barr机制，使QCD真空角在树图级别为零。

Result: 带电费米子质量层级、中微子质量、CKM相位可在一个共同框架内解释；QCD真空角在树图级别为零；超出树图级别的EDM约束要求SM二重态与矢量样扇区的汤川耦合适度压低；若高维算符不受压制，可导致新阿贝尔对称性破缺标度较低，矢量样费米子可达TeV量级。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个简单且统一的方案，能够在标准模型的共同扩展中同时解释带电费米子质量层级、中微子质量和CP破坏参数的起源。

Abstract: We propose a framework for fermion mass generation based on a universal seesaw. The Standard Model is extended by an Abelian gauge symmetry acting on right-handed fermions, together with vector-like fermions and scalar fields. The ordinary Yukawa couplings are forbidden, except for the top-quark coupling to the Higgs, which is allowed at the renormalizable level and remains unsuppressed. The charged-fermion hierarchies then arise from mixing with the vector-like sector, and light neutrino masses emerge from a neutral sector Majorana seesaw. CP is exact in the ultraviolet and broken spontaneously by scalar vacuum expectation values. The resulting CP-violating phase is transmitted to the quark sector and generates the CKM phase, while a Nelson-Barr structure, realized through the universal seesaw block form, keeps the physical QCD vacuum angle zero at tree level. Consistency with EDM bounds beyond tree level requires moderately suppressed Yukawa couplings between SM doublets to the vector-like sector. If the leading higher-dimensional operators are unsuppressed, the same requirement can favor a low breaking scale for the new Abelian symmetry. In this regime the vector-like fermions can lie at the TeV scale, with suppressed mixing with the electroweak sector. This framework provides a simple setting in which the hierarchies of charged-fermion masses, neutrino masses, and CP-violating parameters can be accounted for within a common extension of the Standard Model.

</details>


### [115] [Azimuthal momentum isotropization in the Quark-Gluon Plasma thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07796)
*Sergio Barrera Cabodevila,Xiaojian Du,Carlos A. Salgado,Bin Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在玻尔兹曼方程扩散近似（BEDA）框架下研究了重离子碰撞中初始状态方位角各向异性的弛豫过程，发现各阶谐波系数存在明确的弛豫时间层级，且pT依赖的谐波系数随时间演化呈现初始峰向高动量移动的特征，与实验数据定性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史上认为初始状态方位角各向异性会因末态相互作用迅速消失而被忽视，但在小系统碰撞中描述方位角关联时可能很重要，因此需要系统研究这些初始各向异性的弛豫行为。

Method: 采用玻尔兹曼方程扩散近似（BEDA）理论框架，分析各阶谐波系数的弛豫时间，并模拟pT依赖的谐波系数随时间演化过程。

Result: 发现各阶谐波系数存在明确的弛豫时间层级结构；pT依赖的谐波系数初始峰随时间向高动量区域移动，该特征在唯象模拟中与实验数据吻合。

Conclusion: 初始方位角各向异性在小系统碰撞中不可忽略，其在BEDA框架下的弛豫行为具有系统性的层级特征，pT依赖的演化可作为理解实验观测的重要理论依据。

Abstract: Azimuthal anisotropies coming from the initial state of a heavy-ion collision have been historically disregarded in the study of thermalization because they are expected to be rapidly washed out due to final-state interactions. However, they may be important when one attempts to describe azimuthal correlations observed in the collisions of small systems. In this work, we study how these initial anisotropies relax in the context of the Boltzmann Equation in Diffusion Approximation (BEDA). We find a clear hierarchy in the relaxation time of the anisotropies in terms of each harmonic coefficient. We also explore the evolution of the $p_T$-dependent harmonic coefficients in time, finding a shift in the initial peak towards higher momenta that mimics the experimental data when we perform a phenomenologically motivated simulation.

</details>


### [116] [Impact of QED Radiation on SMEFT Constraints in Deep Inelastic Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07811)
*Sonny Mantry,Jian-Wei Qiu,Jia-Yue Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度非弹性散射（DIS）中QED辐射对标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）约束的影响，发现QED辐射会显著修正DIS截面（量级修正），但对纵向电子自旋不对称性的影响仅为百分之几，后者因此成为更鲁棒的SMEFT观测量。文章给出了SoLID和EIC的基准投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DIS作为超越标准模型物理的强探针，在SMEFT框架下进行精密测量时，需要准确评估碰撞诱导的QED辐射效应对SMEFT约束的影响，以确保未来实验分析的可靠性。

Method: 基于联合QCD+QED因子化框架，利用轻子分布函数和碎裂函数，研究QED辐射对DIS截面和纵向电子自旋不对称性的修正，并在SoLID和EIC上给出基准投影。

Result: QED辐射对DIS截面产生量级修正，但对纵向电子自旋不对称性的影响仅为百分之几；在部分运动学区域，有效动量转移被显著改变。

Conclusion: 纵向电子自旋不对称性相较于截面是更鲁棒的SMEFT观测量；未来精密DIS分析（如SoLID和EIC）需要纳入QED辐射修正以准确提取SMEFT约束。

Abstract: Deep-inelastic scattering (DIS) provides a powerful probe of physics beyond the Standard Model through precision measurements interpreted within the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT). We study the impact of collision-induced QED radiation on SMEFT constraints using the joint QCD+QED factorization framework based on lepton distribution and fragmentation functions. QED radiation can substantially modify DIS cross sections and, in some kinematic regions, significantly alter the effective momentum transfer relevant for factorization. We find that while cross sections receive order-one corrections, longitudinal electron spin asymmetries are affected only at the few-percent level, making them significantly more robust observables for SMEFT studies. Benchmark projections for SoLID and the Electron-Ion Collider are provided to demonstrate the impact of QED radiation for future precision DIS analyses and the extraction of SMEFT constraints.

</details>


### [117] [Neutrino Masses and Dark Matter Stability in 3HDMs with Minimal Non-Abelian Discrete Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07853)
*Cesar Bonilla,Andres Layana-Ramirez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于最小非阿贝尔离散对称群（S₃和D₄）的三-Higgs-二重态模型，同时解释中微子质量和暗物质稳定性。残余Z₂宇称稳定暗物质候选者，通过单圈图产生中微子质量，同时存在树图I型跷跷板贡献。模型在汤川和标量扇区均保持CP守恒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中微子质量和暗物质问题缺乏统一的对称性解释框架，需要寻找同时解决这两个问题的极小模型。

Method: 构建基于最小非阿贝尔离散群（S₃和D₄）的三-Higgs-二重态模型，利用自发对称破缺产生残余Z₂宇称稳定暗物质，采用单圈图与树图I型跷跷板结合的方式产生中微子质量。

Result: 确定S₃和D₄为实现该框架的最小非阿贝尔群，并找到与当前中微子振荡数据一致的极小模型，模型在汤川和标量扇区均保持CP守恒。

Conclusion: 基于S₃和D₄对称性的最小三-Higgs-二重态模型能同时解释中微子质量和暗物质稳定性，为超出标准模型的新物理提供了优雅的极小框架。

Abstract: We present minimal three-Higgs-doublet models (3HDMs) based on global non-Abelian discrete symmetries that simultaneously explain neutrino masses and dark matter stability. A residual $Z_2$ parity from the spontaneous breaking of the new symmetry stabilizes the dark matter candidate, which runs in the loop generating neutrino masses at one loop alongside a tree-level type-I seesaw contribution. We identify $S_3$ and $D_4$ as the smallest non-Abelian groups realizing this framework and determine the minimal models in both cases that are consistent with current neutrino oscillation data. The resulting models conserve CP in both the Yukawa and scalar sectors.

</details>


### [118] [Lepton mixing from the $Δ(96)$ Modular Littlest Seesaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07865)
*Hai-Zhi Hao,Li-Na Yan,Xiang-Gan Liu,Cai-Chang Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次对基于有限模群Δ(96)的Modular Littlest Seesaw模型进行了全面且模型无关的研究。通过构建所有不可约表示的矢量值模形式、分类对称性保持不动点、导出低阶三重态VVMF的对齐方式，对带电轻子、大气中微子和太阳中微子扇区的剩余对称性进行了穷举扫描，识别出35个现象学可行的对称性破缺模式（21个正序、14个反序）。所得Dirac中微子质量矩阵超越了传统CSD(n)结构，产生了新的固定PMNS列和超越TM₁范式的混合参数关联。这些模型具有高预测性，对中微子质量、混合参数和CP相位给出狭窄范围，可供JUNO、DUNE和T2HK等实验严格检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中微子质量模型（如CSD(n)和TM₁混合模式）虽能部分解释实验数据，但仍缺乏基于有限模群Δ(96)的全面、模型无关的理论研究。已有工作多针对特定对称性破缺路径，未系统扫描所有可能的剩余对称性组合及其现象学后果，无法全面探索超越传统范式的理论可能性。

Method: 1. 构建模群Δ(96)所有不可约表示的矢量值模形式（VVMF）；2. 分类对称性保持不动点并导出低阶和次低阶三重态VVMF的对齐方式；3. 在带电轻子、大气中微子和太阳中微子扇区中穷举扫描所有可能的剩余对称性组合；4. 针对每个破缺模式构建Seesaw模型，推导Dirac质量矩阵、PMNS矩阵及混合参数，并检查现象学可行性。

Result: 识别出35个现象学可行的对称性破缺模式（21个正序、14个反序）。得到的Dirac中微子质量矩阵超越了常规CSD(n)结构，产生新的固定PMNS列，并发现超越TM₁范式的轻子混合参数之间新的关联。各可行模型对中微子质量、混合角和CP相位给出狭窄预测范围。

Conclusion: 基于Δ(96)的Modular Littlest Seesaw模型具有高度预测性，提供了一系列超越传统范式的新理论方案。所得预测的狭窄范围可由JUNO、DUNE和T2HK等下一代中微子实验严格检验，为区分不同对称性破缺路径提供了实验判据。

Abstract: We perform the first comprehensive and model independent study of Modular Littlest Seesaw models based on the finite modular group $Δ(96)$. We construct the vector-valued modular forms (VVMFs) for all irreducible representations of modular $Δ(96)$, classify the inequivalent symmetry-preserving fixed points, and derive the corresponding alignments of the low-weight and next-to-lowest-weight triplet VVMFs. These results allow an exhaustive scan over the residual symmetries in the charged lepton, atmospheric neutrino, and solar neutrino sectors. We identify 35 phenomenologically viable and inequivalent breaking patterns, including 21 with normal ordering and 14 with inverted ordering. The resulting Dirac neutrino mass matrices go beyond the conventional CSD$(n)$ structure, yielding new fixed PMNS columns and novel correlations among the lepton mixing parameters beyond the TM$_1$ paradigm. The viable models are highly predictive, giving narrow ranges for neutrino masses, mixing parameters and CP phases, and can be stringently tested by upcoming experiments such as JUNO, DUNE and T2HK.

</details>


### [119] [QCD for electroweak precision measurements: Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07908)
*George Sterman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了标准模型中强相互作用与电弱相互作用之间的耦合关系，追溯了从QED作为精确理论开始，通过深度非弹性散射进入强相互作用领域，再到轻子湮灭和强子-强子散射中的夸克-反夸克湮灭的历史进程。由此对QCD的理解帮助建立了标准模型，同样的技术也构成了高能精确电弱测量和寻找新物理迹象的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过追溯标准模型中强相互作用与电弱相互作用相互耦合和相互促进的历史，展示两者如何利用彼此的知识共同发展，并为精确电弱测量和新物理探索奠定基础。

Method: 采用历史综述的方法，系统梳理从QED精确理论出发，经过深度非弹性散射、轻子湮灭、强子-强子散射中的夸克-反夸克湮灭等关键实验和技术手段，逐步建立对QCD的理解，并最终应用于精确电弱测量。

Result: 对QCD的深入理解帮助建立了标准模型，并且同样的技术方法成为高能精确电弱测量和寻找新物理迹象的有效工具。

Conclusion: 标准模型中强与电弱两个扇区的耦合关系使得相互探索成为可能，历史发展表明这一跨扇区的研究范式是成功的，并将继续推动精确测量和新物理探索。

Abstract: The couplings of the strong to electroweak sectors of the Standard Model enable the exploration of each using our growing knowledge of the other. In this review, we will follow the sweep of history. Starting with QED as a precision theory, deep inelastic scattering served as a gateway to the strong interactions, followed by leptonic annihilation and quark-antiquark annihilation in hadron-hadron scattering. In turn, the resulting understanding of QCD helped establish the Standard Model. The same techniques form the basis for many precision electroweak measurements at high energy and searches for signs of new physics.

</details>


### [120] [Serendipitous supersymmetric solution to the strong CP problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07921)
*Howard Baer,Vernon Barger,Dibyashree Sengupta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出MSSM存在三个问题（μ项、R宇称破坏、五维质子衰变算符），提出用无反常离散Z_n^R对称性统一解决，并通过Kim-Nilles机制再生μ项，最终自然得到DFSZ轴子暗物质候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MSSM存在μ项需要被禁止后在弱尺度再生、R宇称破坏导致质子快速衰变、以及允许五维质子衰变算符三个问题，传统的R宇称或物质宇称P_M只能解决第二个问题，因此需要寻找能同时解决所有问题的对称性方案。

Method: 采用与大统一一致的无反常离散Z_n^R对称性禁止μ项和所有违规算符；利用Kim-Nilles机制将希格斯场与带电PQ荷的规范单态场X、Y耦合，在超对称破缺后于弱尺度再生μ项，同时破缺离散Z_n^R和U(1)_{PQ}对称性。

Result: 破缺的整体U(1)_{PQ}产生赝戈尔斯通玻色子即DFSZ轴子，解决了强CP问题；超对称框架下自然得到SUSY DFSZ轴子作为暗物质候选者，根据诱导的R宇称破坏算符阶数，可能（但非必然）还得到WIMP暗物质候选者。

Conclusion: 基于无反常离散Z_n^R对称性可统一解决MSSM的μ项、质子衰变问题，并自然导出DFSZ轴子解决强CP问题，同时提供SUSY轴子暗物质候选者，是一种理论上自洽且优雅的超对称扩展方案。

Abstract: The Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (MSSM) has several problems: 1. its mu term must be forbidden, then regenerated at the weak scale, 2. it allows for R-parity violating superpotential terms which lead to rapid proton decay, 3. it allows for dimension-5 proton decay operators. The usual imposition of R- or matter parity P_M solves only the second of these, whereas anomaly-free discrete Z_n^R symmetries (consistent with grand unification) address all of them. Once the mu-term is forbidden, the MSSM develops an accidental global U(1)_{PQ} symmetry. By coupling the Higgs fields to PQ-charged gauge singlet fields X, Y (in the Kim-Nilles mechanism), and imposing SUSY breaking, one regenerates mu at the weak scale whilst breaking the discrete Z_n^R and the U(1)_{PQ}. The broken global U(1)_{PQ} develops a pseudo-Goldstone boson, the DFSZ axion, thus (perhaps inadvertently) solving the strong CP problem. In this setting, SUSY develops a dark matter candidate, the SUSY DFSZ axion, and possibly, though not necessarily, a WIMP dark matter candidate as well, depending on the order of the induced R-parity violating operators.

</details>


### [121] [Multipolar Dark Matter Freeze-out in an Early Matter-Dominated Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07956)
*Debajit Bose,Prolay Chanda,Suvam Maharana,Poulami Mondal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了早期物质主导宇宙中，通过高维电磁算符相互作用的费米子暗物质的冻结过程，发现熵稀释效应可显著降低所需相互作用强度，使原本被排除的参数空间变得可行，表明大爆炸核合成前的宇宙学历史对电磁多极暗物质现象学有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准辐射主导冻结场景下，电磁多极暗物质受到直接探测实验和太阳中微子观测的严格约束，参数空间受限。作者旨在探究早期物质主导宇宙（伴随熵注入）能否放宽这些约束，为暗物质模型提供新的可行参数区域。

Method: 考虑磁偶极、电偶极、anapole和电荷半径四种电磁相互作用，计算在早期物质主导宇宙中来自长寿命重场衰变的熵注入效应下，暗物质冻结所需的耦合强度，并将结果与标准辐射主导场景及当前直接探测和太阳中微子实验约束进行比较。

Result: 早期物质主导时期的熵稀释效应显著降低了重现观测暗物质 relic丰度所需的相互作用强度，使得在标准宇宙学历史中被排除的参数空间区域变得可行。

Conclusion: 大爆炸核合成前的宇宙学历史对电磁多极暗物质的现象学和实验可行性具有重要影响，考虑非标准宇宙学扩展了暗物质模型的存活参数空间。

Abstract: The relic abundance of thermal dark matter depends not only on its particle interactions but also on the expansion history of the early Universe. We study the freeze-out of fermionic dark matter interacting with the Standard Model through higher-dimensional electromagnetic operators in an early matter-dominated cosmology. In particular, we consider magnetic dipole, electric dipole, anapole, and charge-radius interactions, and compute the couplings required to reproduce the observed dark matter relic abundance in the presence of entropy injection from the decay of a long-lived heavy field. The resulting parameter space is compared with that obtained in the standard radiation-dominated freeze-out scenario and confront it with current constraints from direct-detection experiments and solar neutrino observations. We find that the entropy dilution associated with an early matter-dominated epoch significantly reduces the interaction strength required to obtain the observed relic abundance, thereby rendering viable regions of parameter space that are excluded in the conventional cosmological history. Our results demonstrate that the cosmological history prior to Big Bang nucleosynthesis can have an important impact on the phenomenology and experimental viability of electromagnetic multipole dark matter.

</details>


### [122] [Experimental access to the gluonic origin of the proton mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07969)
*Zein-Eddine Meziani,Ismail Zahed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了质子质量的主要来源不是希格斯机制，而是QCD真空的量子结构，并提出了通过标量引力形状因子实验探测胶子迹线反常贡献的直接实验途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明质子质量的起源，特别是QCD真空中的胶子迹线反常对质子质量的支配性贡献，并建立其与可观测物理量的联系。

Method: 通过标量引力形状因子重构胶子迹线形状因子，利用深度虚康普顿散射（夸克标量引力形状因子）、近阈重夸克偶素产生（胶子标量引力形状因子）以及核子西格玛项形状因子三个可测量量进行重建，并与格点QCD和瞬子液体模型进行定量比较。

Result: 从迹线反常中提取的胶子标量形状因子在强子距离尺度上与格点QCD和瞬子液体模型定量一致，验证了该实验路径的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了直接探测可见质量胶子起源的实验途径，揭示了质子质量主要源于QCD共形对称性破缺引起的胶子迹线反常。

Abstract: Most of the proton mass originates not from the Higgs mechanism but from the quantum structure of the QCD vacuum. The dominant contribution arises from the gluonic trace anomaly associated with the breaking of conformal symmetry in quantum chromodynamics. We show that this anomaly contribution is experimentally accessible through scalar gravitational form factors. The key observable is the scalar gluonic trace form factor of the proton, which can be reconstructed from three measurable quantities: the quark scalar gravitational form factor accessible in deeply virtual Compton scattering, the gluon scalar gravitational form factor measurable in near-threshold heavy quarkonium production, and the nucleon sigma-term form factor. We also show that the scalar gluonic form factor extracted from the trace anomaly is quantitatively consistent with lattice QCD and the instanton liquid model over hadronic distance scales. These results provide a direct experimental path to probing the gluonic origin of visible mass.

</details>


### [123] [Study of exotic hadron states in the $DD^{*}$ system via the complex momentum representation and Green's function method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08040)
*Di Wu,Mao Song,Jian-You Guo,Gang Li,Xuan Luo,Peng Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合投影算符方法、复动量表象方法和格林函数的综合框架，用于统一描述DD*系统中的束缚态、共振态和连续谱，并将X(3872)、T_cc^+、Z_c(3900)解释为束缚态，G(3900)解释为P波共振态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以对强子分子态中的束缚态、共振态和连续谱进行统一描述，缺乏一个能够全面分析DD*系统散射动力学的理论框架。

Method: 采用投影算符方法导出DD*系统的动量空间相互作用势，结合复动量表象方法和格林函数，计算散射相移和截面，实现束缚态、共振态和连续谱的统一描述。

Result: 在强子分子态框架下，X(3872)、T_cc^+和Z_c(3900)被一致地解释为束缚态，G(3900)被解释为P波共振态；散射相移和截面的分解有助于理解共振态和连续谱的作用。

Conclusion: 该综合框架为分析DD*系统的散射动力学提供了有效途径，能够统一描述强子分子态中不同类型的奇特强子态。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel approach to investigate exotic hadronic states. For the $DD^{*}$ system, we employ the projection operator method to derive the momentum-space interaction potential. Subsequently, the complex momentum representation (CMR) method is adopted to realize a unified description of bound states, resonant states, and the continuum. By combining the Green's function and the CMR, the scattering phase shifts and cross sections are determined. This integrated approach provides a comprehensive framework for analyzing the scattering dynamics of the $DD^{*}$ system. In the hadronic molecular state framework, the $X(3872)$, $T_{cc}^+$, and $Z_c(3900)$ states can be consistently explained as bound states, while the $G(3900)$ can be interpreted as a $P$-wave resonant state. The decomposition of the scattering phase shifts and cross sections facilitates understanding the roles of resonant and continuum spectrum.

</details>


### [124] [$Z^\prime$ Portal Dark Matter with Observable $ΔN_{\rm eff}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08082)
*Ang Liu,Zhi-Long Han,Fei Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究U(1)_{B-L}对称性下Z' portal暗物质模型，考虑Dirac型右手中微子ν_R对有效相对论物种数ΔN_eff的贡献，分别在WIMP和FIMP两种暗物质产生机制下，系统探索了满足ΔN_eff、微扰限制、直接/间接探测及Z'对撞机搜索等约束的可行参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规Z' portal暗物质模型通常假设Majorana型右手中微子，但若ν_R为Dirac型，其会对ΔN_eff产生额外贡献，从而改变Z' portal暗物质的可探测性预测。随着未来实验对ΔN_eff测量精度的显著提升，有必要重新审视该模型并探索其参数空间。

Method: 在U(1)_{B-L}对称性框架下，构建包含Dirac型ν_R（U(1)_{B-L}电荷Q_{ν_R}=-1）和任意Q_χ的暗物质χ的模型。基于χ与ν_R的产生关联，分别考虑WIMP和FIMP两种Z' portal暗物质产生机制，系统扫描参数空间，施加来自热和非热ν_R诱导的ΔN_eff约束、微扰性限制、直接/间接探测以及Z'对撞机搜索等多项限制。

Result: 得到了在ΔN_eff、微扰性、直接/间接探测及对撞机搜索联合约束下，WIMP和FIMP两种Z' portal暗物质模型的可行参数空间区域，明确了Dirac型ν_R对参数空间的新限制，特别是ΔN_eff对未来实验敏感性的影响。

Conclusion: Dirac型右手中微子显著改变了Z' portal暗物质模型的可探测性预测，未来ΔN_eff的精确测量将成为区分不同中微子类型及约束参数空间的关键探针。

Abstract: In the conventional $Z^\prime$ portal dark matter scenario, the prediction of detectable dark matter $χ$ typically relies on the collider sensitivities of $Z^\prime$ and direct detection, where the Majorana type right-handed neutrinos are usually assumed. However, if the right-handed neutrinos $ν_R$ are Dirac type, they will contribute to the additional effective number of relativistic species $ΔN_{\rm eff}$, which brings different detectable predictions for $Z^\prime$ portal dark matter. In light of the great improvement of $ΔN_{\rm eff}$ for the upcoming experiments, we investigate the $Z^\prime$ portal dark matter with Dirac type $ν_R$. Under the $U(1)_{B-L}$ symmetry, this model includes $ν_R$ with $U(1)_{B-L}$ charge $Q_{ν_R}=-1$ and $χ$ with arbitrary $Q_χ$ beyond the SM. Based on the relation in the production of $χ$ and $ν_R$, both the WIMP and FIMP dark matter through the $Z^\prime$ portal scenario are considered. We perform a comprehensive exploration of the viable parameter space under the constraints from $ΔN_{\rm eff}$ induced by thermal and non-thermal $ν_R$, perturbative limit, dark matter direct and indirect detection, and collider searches of $Z^\prime$.

</details>


### [125] [Overview of Applications of Quantum Computing in QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08169)
*Germán Rodrigo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了量子计算在粒子对撞机物理中的应用，涵盖事件生成、部分子簇射模拟、散射振幅计算、圈积分与相空间积分等方向，讨论了相较经典方法的潜在优势以及当前含噪中等规模量子硬件的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算有望解决对撞机物理中经典计算难以处理的高复杂度问题，推动高能物理模拟与数据分析的突破。

Method: 综述近年来提出的多种量子算法，系统梳理其在事件生成、部分子簇射模拟、散射振幅、圈与相空间积分、实验优化问题等方向的应用思路。

Result: 量子方法在某些计算环节展现出潜在加速优势，但当前NISQ硬件限制了实际量子优势的实现。

Conclusion: 量子计算为对撞机物理提供了新范式，但距离实际超越经典计算仍需硬件与算法的协同发展。

Abstract: Quantum computing has emerged as a promising framework for addressing computationally demanding problems in collider physics. In recent years, a growing number of quantum algorithms have been proposed for applications ranging from event generation and parton shower simulation to the evaluation of scattering amplitudes, loop and phase-space integration, and optimization problems relevant to experimental analysis. We provide a concise overview of the main ideas behind these developments, with emphasis on the potential advantages of quantum approaches in comparison with classical methods, as well as on the current limitations imposed by noisy intermediate-scale quantum hardware.

</details>


### [126] [Transformer-based machine learning using low-level calorimeter signals for collimated photon identification at collider experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08175)
*Gabriel Matos,Lauren Larson,Abhilasha Dave,Maria Bressan,Azal Amer,Cindy Liu,Nikiforos Nikiforou,Jonathan Long,Timothy Andeen,John Parsons,Julia Gonski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用新型电磁量能器的精细颗粒度信息，通过细胞级机器学习方法（特别是Transformer架构）对高度准直的双光子信号（来自类轴子粒子衰变）与孤立单光子簇射进行分类，并实现不变质量回归，显著优于传统基于簇射形状变量的高能物理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁量能器包含丰富的簇射内部结构信息，但传统高能重建方法会丢弃大量细胞级细节。同时，轻类轴子粒子衰变产生的高度准直双光子信号难以与传统单光子信号区分，需要更精细的分类与重建方法。

Method: 系统比较了多种机器学习架构（共六种），包括基于高能簇射形状变量的方法和直接基于细胞级信息的方法，其中Transformer架构表现最佳，MLP Mixer作为资源受限场景的替代方案。

Result: 细胞级机器学习方法在分类能力上显著优于传统方法；Transformer在六种架构中表现最优；Transformer还可直接从量能器细胞进行不变质量回归，改善对轻共振态的表征，并减少π⁰和η假光子背景。

Conclusion: 细胞级机器学习方法可大幅拓展基于量能器的粒子鉴别能力，超越现有常规技术的性能极限。

Abstract: Electromagnetic calorimeters provide essential information for reconstructing and selecting both Standard Model (SM) and potential beyond the SM physics events at high-energy particle colliders. The fine-grained segmentation of modern calorimeters captures rich information about the internal structure of particle showers, much of which is discarded by conventional high-level reconstruction methods. In this work, we leverage calorimeter cell-level information to classify highly collimated diphoton signatures, arising from the decay of light axion-like particles, from isolated single-photon showers. We systematically compare a range of machine learning architectures, spanning high-level, shower shape variable-based approaches and direct cell-level methods. Cell-level machine learning shows significantly superior classification ability, with a Transformer in particular representing the best performance among six different architectures studied, and an MLP Mixer representing a resource-constrained alternative for potential real-time, trigger-level applications. Beyond classification, the Transformer model developed enables direct invariant mass regression from calorimeter cells, improving the characterization of light resonances and providing an additional handle in reducing the $π^0$ and $η$ fake photon backgrounds. These results demonstrate that cell-level machine learning methods can extend calorimeter-based particle identification and performance well beyond the capabilities of current conventional techniques.

</details>


### [127] [Probing Quadratically Coupled Ultralight Dark Matter with the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08248)
*Xucheng Gan,Hyungjin Kim,Anna-Malin Lemke,Andrea Mitridate*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了与标准模型发生二次耦合的超轻暗物质在未来空间引力波探测器LISA中产生的信号，发现信号出现在两个特征频率上，LISA在特定质量范围内可超越当前实验的约束，且不受屏蔽效应影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻暗物质可通过引力及非引力相互作用与标准模型粒子耦合，在引力波实验中留下独特信号。当前对这类暗物质的探测受限于地面实验的屏蔽效应和灵敏度，因此需要探索新的探测途径。

Method: 考虑超轻暗物质与标准模型的二次耦合，分析其在LISA探测器中产生的信号特征。由于耦合的二次性质，信号出现在两个不同频率：两倍暗物质质量对应频率以及低于暗物质典型动能对应的频率。

Result: LISA在特定质量范围内能够超越当前地面和天体物理探测手段对超轻暗物质的约束。此外，LISA中的暗物质信号不受屏蔽效应影响，而屏蔽效应正是限制地面实验灵敏度的关键因素。

Conclusion: LISA作为空间引力波探测器，对二次耦合超轻暗物质具有独特的探测能力，可以在特定质量区间提供比现有实验更强的约束，且避免了地面实验的屏蔽限制。

Abstract: Ultralight dark matter can interact with Standard Model particles via gravitational and non-gravitational interactions. Through such interactions, it can leave distinctive signals in gravitational-wave experiments. In this work, we investigate signals induced by ultralight dark matter quadratically coupled to the Standard Model in the future space-borne gravitational-wave detector, the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). Due to the quadratic nature of the coupling, dark matter signals appear at two distinct frequencies: the frequency corresponding to twice the dark matter mass, and frequencies below the typical dark matter kinetic energy. We analyze both contributions and show that LISA can surpass current constraints from terrestrial and astrophysical probes in certain mass ranges. We also find that dark matter signals in LISA are free from screening effects which significantly limit the sensitivity of terrestrial experiments.

</details>


### [128] [Precision three-Dimensional Atmospheric Neutrino Flux Calculation Based on Honda Flux Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08310)
*Jie Cheng,Yu-Feng Li,Liang-Jian Wen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Honda框架，首次引入地球内μ子传播及其衰变/核捕获过程，结合AMS02初级宇宙线模型、IGRF2020地磁场和μ子校准的强子相互作用模型，计算了七个探测器位点的三维大气中微子通量（10 MeV~10^4 GeV），揭示了低能区显著的位点依赖性，量化了μ子传播对低能中微子通量的全球性贡献，并显著降低了强子相互作用系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大气中微子通量计算未完整考虑地球内μ子传播及其衰变/核捕获过程对低能中微子通量的贡献，且强子相互作用模型的不确定性较大，亟需更精确的通量计算以支持新一代中微子振荡实验和暗物质直接探测实验（如中微子雾问题）。

Method: 基于Honda三维模拟框架，首次纳入地球内μ子传播（包括衰变和核捕获）；采用AMS02宇宙线模型、IGRF2020地磁场模型、μ子校准的强子相互作用模型；覆盖七个不同地磁环境的探测器位点，能量范围10 MeV~10^4 GeV。

Result: 低能区（<10 GeV）通量呈现显著位点依赖性，1 GeV以下IceCube的ν_μ通量约为JUNO的两倍；与HKKMS15相比存在2%-10%偏差；μ子传播对低能中微子通量有全局性贡献，且绝对增量近似与位点无关；更新后的强子模型显著降低了系统误差。

Conclusion: 该计算结果为JUNO、Super-Kamiokande/Hyper-Kamiokande、DUNE、KM3NeT/ORCA、IceCube等中微子实验以及面临中微子雾的暗物质直接探测实验提供了关键的输入数据。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive three-dimensional atmospheric neutrino flux calculation based on the well-recognized simulation framework develeped by Honda and his collaborators, incorporating for the first time the muon propagation inside the Earth and its subsequent decay or nuclear capture. Other updates of essential input models include: the AMS02-based primary cosmic ray model, IGRF2020 geomagnetic field, and muon-recalibrated hadronic interaction model. The calculation covers seven detector sites across diverse geomagnetic environments, spanning 10~MeV to $10^4$~GeV. Significant site-dependent differences appear at $E_ν< 10$~GeV, with $ν_μ$ flux at IceCube approximately twice that at JUNO below 1~GeV. Compared to HKKMS15, deviations of 2\%--10\% are attributed to the updated input models. Below 100~MeV, we present precise flux results, revealing that muon propagation contributes a globally significant component to the low-energy neutrino flux at all sites, with an approximately site-independent absolute increment. The hadronic uncertainty is re-estimated across the energy range using the updated hadronic interaction model, with significant reduction of the systematic error compared to previous calculations. These results provide essential inputs for neutrino oscillation and rare-event search experiments including JUNO, Super-Kamiokande/Hyper-Kamiokande, DUNE, KM3NeT/ORCA, and IceCube, as well as direct dark matter detection experiments facing the neutrino fog.

</details>


### [129] [Revisiting One-Zero and Two-Zero Neutrino Mass Textures in Light of Recent Oscillation and Cosmological Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08384)
*Haruto Kitagawa,Coh Miyao,Satsuki Nishimura,Hajime Otsuka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在最新实验和宇宙学约束下重新审视中微子质量矩阵的一零和双零纹理结构，发现双零纹理中B系列在仅考虑CMB约束时仍允许，而加入BAO约束后仅A系列可行；利用流匹配机器学习技术分析一零纹理，发现部分结构已被排除，允许的结构对中微子质量总和、有效电子中微子质量、无中微子双贝塔衰变有效质量及狄拉克CP相位有独特预测，并讨论了一零纹理可能源于非可逆选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在最新中微子振荡参数、宇宙学质量总和约束、运动学有效电子中微子质量界限及无中微子双贝塔衰变限制下，重新评估中微子质量矩阵的一零和双零纹理结构的现象学可行性，以缩小理论参数空间并指导未来实验。

Method: 基于当前中微子振荡实验、宇宙微波背景（CMB）及重子声学振荡（BAO）数据、运动学界限和无中微子双贝塔衰变实验的最新结果，对中微子质量矩阵一零和双零纹理进行参数扫描与可行性筛选；对一零纹理采用流匹配（flow matching）机器学习技术进行参数空间采样与预测。

Result: 双零纹理中，仅施加CMB限制时B系列纹理仍允许，其特征为狄拉克CP相位δ_CP约在π/2和3π/2附近；加入更强CMB+BAO约束后，仅A系列纹理存活。一零纹理中部分结构已被当前数据排除，允许的结构对中微子质量总和、有效电子中微子质量、无中微子双贝塔衰变有效质量和δ_CP给出区分性预测。

Conclusion: 当前宇宙学约束对中微子质量纹理结构具有强甄别能力；双零纹理中B系列纹理可在未来无中微子双贝塔衰变实验中被检验；一零纹理的允许结构可通过非可逆选择规则从紫外理论中实现，为理论构建提供新方向。

Abstract: We revisit one-zero and two-zero textures of the neutrino mass matrix under current experimental and cosmological constraints. We identify the phenomenologically viable texture structures using the latest results on neutrino oscillation parameters, the cosmological bound on the sum of neutrino masses, the kinematic bound on the effective electron-neutrino mass, and limits from neutrinoless double-beta decay. For two-zero textures, several structures are still allowed if only the CMB bound on the neutrino mass sum is imposed. Among them, the $B$-series textures show a characteristic prediction for the Dirac CP phase, with $δ_{\rm CP}$ lying around $π/2$ and $3π/2$, and are within the reach of future neutrinoless double-beta decay searches. When the stronger CMB+BAO constraint is included, however, only the $A$-series textures remain viable. Therefore, we also analyze one-zero textures by using machine learning techniques, particularly flow matching. It turns out that some of the texture structures are already excluded by current data, while the allowed ones give distinct predictions for $\sum_i m_i$, $m_{ν_e}^{\rm eff}$, $\langle m_{ee}\rangle$, and $δ_{\rm CP}$. We further discuss how the one-zero texture structures can arise from non-invertible selection rules.

</details>


### [130] [Electron-Ion Collision Environment: Distribution of Quark Spin and Orbital Angular Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08457)
*Sujit Jana,Ashutosh Dwibedi,Vikash Kumar Ojha,Sabyasachi Ghosh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Nambu--Jona-Lasinio模型在有限核密度下计算夸克质量，进而通过光前缀饰夸克模型计算质子与原子核的广义横向动量依赖部分子分布（GTMD），并引入eP与eA碰撞的GTMD比值作为核密度效应的间接测量手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来电子-离子对撞机（EIC）将能同时在质子（eP）和原子核（eA）碰撞中测量部分子分布，这需要从理论上研究质子和原子核内部夸克自旋与轨道角动量的分布差异。

Method: 采用Nambu--Jona-Lasinio（NJL）模型在零核密度和接近核饱和密度下确定组分夸克质量；基于光前缀饰夸克模型计算与夸克轨道角动量、自旋及自旋-轨道关联相关的GTMD；引入类似重离子碰撞中核抑制因子的eP与eA碰撞GTMD比值。

Result: 得到了不同核密度条件下的组分夸克质量，以及相应的夸克OAM、自旋和自旋-轨道关联的GTMD分布；定义了eP与eA碰撞的GTMD比值，其偏离1的程度可反映非微扰QCD的多体核密度效应。

Conclusion: 所提出的GTMD比值为EIC实验提供了一种间接探测原子核密度效应的方法，有助于区分质子和原子核中夸克自旋与轨道角动量的结构差异。

Abstract: The future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) will enable measurements of the same partonic distributions inside both the proton and the nucleus through electron-proton (eP) and electron-ion (eA) collisions. This capability motivates the present theoretical study of the distributions of quark spin and orbital angular momentum within the proton and the nucleus. To map the eP and eA collision environments, we employ the Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model at finite nuclear density to determine the constituent quark masses at zero nuclear density and near the nuclear saturation density. Using these quark mass inputs, we calculate the generalized transverse momentum-dependent parton distributions (GTMDs) associated with quark orbital angular momentum (OAM), spin, and spin-orbit correlations within the light-front dressed quark model. Furthermore, inspired by the nuclear suppression factor widely used in heavy-ion collision experiments, we introduce a set of GTMD ratios between eP and eA collisions. Any deviation of these ratios from unity provides an indirect measure of many-body nuclear density effects arising from non-perturbative quantum chromodynamics (QCD).

</details>


### [131] [AMFlow 2.0: significant algorithmic and software improvements for Feynman integral evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08477)
*Rui-Jun Huang,Xiao Liu,Yan-Qing Ma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: AMFlow包的重大改进：引入替代递归模式、高性能微分方程求解器、支持最新IBP约化器等新特性，显著降低计算成本，在三圈五点拓扑上验证了符号和数值性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进AMFlow包以减少维度正则化费曼积分数值计算的计算成本，提升计算效率。

Method: 引入替代递归模式、高性能微分方程求解器、支持最先进的积分-by-部分约化器以及其他实用改进。

Result: 在三圈五点拓扑基准测试中，符号和数值性能均得到显著提升。

Conclusion: AMFlow包的新版本在计算效率和性能上实现了重大改进。

Abstract: We present significant improvements to the AMFlow package for the numerical computation of dimensionally regularized Feynman integrals. Several new features are introduced to reduce computational cost, including an alternative recursion mode, a high-performance differential equation solver, support for state-of-the-art integration-by-parts reducers and other useful improvements. We benchmark the new version on a three-loop five-point topology and find that both the symbolic and numerical performance are significantly improved.

</details>


### [132] [Leptogenesis and Low Reheating Temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08663)
*Marcos A. G. Garcia,Stephen E. Henrich,Wenqi Ke,Keith A. Olive*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非瞬时再加热过程中的轻子生成机制，发现对于广义Starobinsky势（k≥4），即使在极低再加热温度下也能产生观测到的重子不对称性，且最终不对称性主要依赖于两个参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非瞬时再加热期间轻子生成的可行性，特别是探索低再加热温度下能否产生观测到的重子不对称性。

Method: 在标准I型跷跷板框架下，考虑来自暴胀子衰变的非热右手中微子产生，详细分析辐射状再加热（w_φ=1/3, k=4）情况，包括暴胀子凝聚体碎裂效应及有效质量演化带来的运动学关闭效应。

Result: 物质状再加热在极低温度下与标准轻子生成不兼容；而对于广义Starobinsky势（k≥4），可在任意低再加热温度（高于BBN界限~4 MeV）下获得观测重子不对称性；最终重子不对称性主要依赖于暴胀子-RHN耦合参数y_φNN和CP破坏参数|ε|，而对RHN质量、再加热温度和RHN衰变率不敏感。

Conclusion: 非瞬时再加热中的轻子生成机制在广义Starobinsky势下具有普适性，且最终不对称性由少数参数决定，这简化了理论预测。

Abstract: We study leptogenesis during non-instantaneous reheating in the canonical type-I seesaw framework, with the dominant source of right-handed neutrino (RHN) production being non-thermal from inflaton decays ($φ\rightarrow NN$). While matter-like reheating ($w_φ=0$) fails to be compatible with standard leptogenesis for very low reheating temperatures, the situation is strikingly different for generalized Starobinsky potentials approximated by $V(φ)\proptoφ^k$ with $k\geq4$ about the minimum. In the latter cases, the observed baryon asymmetry can readily be obtained for arbitrarily low reheating temperatures above the BBN bound of $\sim4$ MeV. We study radiation-like reheating ($w_φ=1/3$, $k=4$) in detail, showing that the evolving effective mass of the inflaton condensate leads to kinematic shutoff of the $φ\rightarrow NN$ channel, which qualitatively changes the leptogenesis dynamics. We include a detailed treatment of the effects of fragmentation of the inflaton condensate. The final baryon asymmetry depends primarily on only two parameters: the inflaton-RHN coupling, $y_{φNN}$, and the CP-violating parameter $|ε|$. Interestingly, the final asymmetry is largely insensitive to the RHN mass, the reheating temperature, and the RHN decay rate. While we focus on fermionic reheating, we show that the general features of these results also hold for bosonic reheating to scalars.

</details>


### [133] [Analysis of Nuclear Fragmentation Functions for Pions with $A$ and $ν$ Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08669)
*Mengyang Li,Zijian Ye,Jun Gao,Xiaomin Shen,Hongxi Xing,Yuxiang Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对π介子核碎裂函数（nFFs）进行了QCD分析，从半包容深度非弹性散射数据中同时提取了真空碎裂函数及其核修正，并参数化核效应与质量数A、部分子能量ν及强子能量分数z的依赖关系。拟合在z∈[0.2,0.7]范围内对nFFs有良好约束，NLO预言与ALICE数据在误差内一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核碎裂函数（nFFs）是描述高能核碰撞中强子化过程核修饰的关键非微扰量，但目前相关QCD分析尚不充分，需要系统提取和参数化这些核效应，以定量理解部分子在核介质中的碎裂机制。

Method: 采用QCD框架对nFFs进行全局分析，同时拟合真空碎裂函数及其核修正；核效应参数化为质量数A、靶静止系部分子能量ν和强子能量分数z的函数；使用选定运动学截断的核靶SIDIS数据，确保pQCD和共线因子化的适用性。

Result: 拟合良好描述大多数数据集，nFFs在z∈[0.2,0.7]范围内受到良好约束；基于新nFFs的NLO预言与ALICE pp及pA碰撞实验数据在现有不确定度内合理一致。

Conclusion: 成功提取了π介子核碎裂函数，实现了核介质中强子化修正的系统参数化，为核环境下的碎裂过程提供了定量描述工具，有助于理解核介质对部分子碎裂的影响。

Abstract: We present a QCD analysis of pion nuclear fragmentation functions (nFFs), which encode nuclear modifications to hadronization in high-energy nuclear collisions. Within this framework, vacuum fragmentation functions and their nuclear modifications are extracted simultaneously. The nuclear effects are parameterized as functions of the mass number $A$, the energy of the fragmenting parton in the target rest frame $ν$, and the hadron energy fraction $z$, allowing their dependence on these variables to be quantified. Our analysis includes semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering data on nuclear targets, with kinematic cuts chosen to ensure the applicability of perturbative QCD and collinear factorization. The resulting fit provides a good description of most datasets, with the nFFs well constrained in the energy fraction range $z \in [0.2, 0.7]$. Additionally, with our new nFFs, we present next-to-leading order predictions in $pp$ and $pA$ collisions, which show reasonable agreement with ALICE data within the current experimental uncertainties.

</details>


### [134] [Heavy quark coalescence probability in the presence of a potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08707)
*Taesoo Song,Jiaxing Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究基于组分夸克模型发展了唯象重夸克势，用于研究重夸克并合过程，发现该势能增强并合概率，且在中等程度介质修正下并合概率仍接近1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究重夸克势在重夸克并合过程中的作用，特别是低动量下并合概率预期为1的机制。

Method: 基于组分夸克模型发展唯象重夸克势，使其能够重现赝标量和矢量重介子的真空质量，并用该势能计算并合概率，同时研究夸克-胶子等离子体中重夸克势的介质修正对并合过程的影响。

Result: 所发展的唯象重夸克势对并合概率具有增强效应；只要重夸克势的介质修正足够温和，并合概率仍保持接近1。

Conclusion: 重夸克势在重夸克并合中起重要作用，合适的势能形式能维持高并合概率，这为理解重味强子在QGP中的形成机制提供了依据。

Abstract: In this study, we explore the role of the heavy quark potential in heavy quark coalescence, whose probability is expected to be unity at low momentum. To this end, we develop a phenomenological heavy quark potential based on the constituent quark model that reproduces the vacuum masses of pseudoscalar and vector heavy mesons. Using this potential, we demonstrate its enhancement effect on the coalescence probability. We also investigate how medium-induced modifications of the heavy quark potential in the quark gluon plasma affect the coalescence process. Our results indicate that the coalescence probability remains close to unity as long as the modification of the potential is sufficiently moderate.

</details>


### [135] [Optimal-Transport-Based Cell Resampling for Negative and Pathological Event Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08723)
*Regan Doherty,Lauren Hay,Rishabh Jain,Matt LeBlanc,Julia Marrinan,Camille Mauceri,Jennifer Roloff*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于最优传输（Optimal Transport）的度量（能量移动距离及其谱变体）在单元重采样算法中的应用，以解决大型强子对撞机实验中负权重和过大蒙特卡洛事件权重问题。这些度量对软共线辐射不敏感，可直接应用于事件生成的任意阶段。在QCD次领头阶模拟中，该方法相比其他重采样技术降低了偏差。本文还提出了截面移动距离作为衡量全相空间重加权偏差的无偏、广泛适用的优值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型强子对撞机实验中，负权重和过大的蒙特卡洛事件权重给计算资源带来巨大压力，需要有效的权重重采样技术来缓解这一问题。

Method: 采用基于最优传输的度量（能量移动距离EMD及其谱变体）作为单元重采样算法的距离度量，利用其对软共线辐射不敏感的特性，直接在任意阶段的事件粒子上应用。

Result: 在QCD次领头阶模拟中，该方法相比文献中其他单元重采样技术显著降低了观测偏差。

Conclusion: 基于最优传输的度量在事件权重重采样中表现优越，且截面移动距离可作为衡量全相空间重加权偏差的通用优值。

Abstract: Negative and pathologically large Monte Carlo event weights strain the computing budgets of experiments at the Large Hadron Collider. Cell resampling algorithms locally redistribute event weights among nearby events in a metric space. We study the performance of metrics defined in terms of Optimal Transport, namely the Energy Mover's Distance and a spectral variant, in the context of such algorithms. As these metrics are insensitive to the addition of soft and collinear radiation, they may be applied directly to particles at any stage of event generation. When applied to samples simulated at next-to-leading-order in quantum chromodynamics, this approach reduces the observed bias relative to other cell resampling techniques presented in the literature. We also study the Cross-Section Mover's Distance as an unbinned, broadly-applicable figure of merit for quantifying the bias introduced by any full-phase-space reweighting.

</details>


### [136] [Internal pseudospin, lepton-number superselection, and neutrino--antineutrino coherence in massive neutral-fermion one-particle states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08739)
*Ricardo Romero*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明有质量狄拉克中微子单粒子态在固定三维动量下张成4维空间，并揭示了由螺旋性翻转、电荷共轭及其乘积构成的内部SU(2)赝自旋子代数（属于SU(4)），区别于Wigner小群。该框架代数化地分类了马约拉纳质量和赝狄拉克分裂，无需假设中微子为马约拉纳粒子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一种代数框架，统一描述狄拉克中微子的螺旋性、电荷共轭和轻子数性质，并分类中微子质量类型（马约拉纳与赝狄拉克），而不预先假设中微子是马约拉纳粒子。

Method: 利用SU(4)生成元τ_μ⊗σ_ν的数学结构，以轻子数L和螺旋性H_h进行分级，分析ΔL=2扇区。通过识别螺旋性保持方向（τ_{1,2}⊗σ_{0,3}）对应赝狄拉克混合，螺旋性翻转方向（τ_{1,2}⊗σ_{1,2}）对应无中微子双贝塔衰变质量因子，并证明电荷共轭匹配U_2生成元。

Result: 发现了狄拉克中微子4维态空间内的内部SU(2)赝自旋子代数；明确了赝狄拉克混合（活性—惰性）与无中微子双贝塔衰变质量因子（活性—活性）分别对应不同生成元方向；证明了马约拉纳条件是向U_2=+1本征空间的投影。

Conclusion: 该代数框架提供了一种无需假设中微子为马约拉纳粒子的方法，即可分类马约拉纳质量和赝狄拉克分裂，为理解中微子质量起源和性质提供了新的代数工具。

Abstract: At fixed three-momentum, massive Dirac neutrino one-particle states span a 4D space of particle--antiparticle identity and helicity. We show that helicity flip, charge conjugation, and their product close an internal $SU(2)$ pseudospin subalgebra within $SU(4)$, distinct from the Wigner little group. Its helicity generator is the lepton-number-weighted spin rotation $U_1=2LJ_2$. The lepton-number $L$ and helicity $\mathcal H_h$ grade the 16 generators $τ_μ\otimesσ_ν$, organizing the $ΔL=2$ sector. Helicity-preserving directions $τ_{1,2}\otimesσ_{0,3}$ carry the pseudo-Dirac mixing $κ_{\rm pD}=Δm^2/4E$ (active--sterile), while helicity-flipping directions $τ_{1,2}\otimesσ_{1,2}$ carry the neutrinoless double-beta decay mass factor (active--active). Furthermore, charge conjugation matches the $U_2$ generator. The Majorana condition is thus a projection onto the $U_2=+1$ eigenspace, where only the Wigner algebra survives. This framework algebraically classifies Majorana masses and pseudo-Dirac splittings without assuming neutrinos are Majorana particles.

</details>
