### [137] [Gravitational perturbations of a regular T-duality inspired black hole: Quasinormal modes, excitation factors, and time-domain evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07715)
*Bekir Can Lütfüoğlu,Mardon Abdullaev,Javlon Rayimbaev,Shokhzod Jumaniyozov,Shavkat Karshiboev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由T-对偶性启发零长导致的规则黑洞的轴向引力扰动，发现零点长度增大时ADM标度下的准正则模实部增加（振荡加快），阻尼率先微增后减小，激发因子变化相对平缓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非局部、T-对偶性启发的零长引力理论所描述的规则黑洞的轴向引力扰动特性，探索与Schwarzschild黑洞的偏差。

Method: 使用高阶WKB-Padé方法计算基本准正则模及多个泛音，并通过时域演化验证主导模；同时计算激发因子。

Result: 零点长度开启后，角指数ℓ=2,3,4的引力模ADM标度频率实部增大（环荡振荡加快）；阻尼率先略增后在高变形参数下减小；激发因子幅度变化远小于频率变化。

Conclusion: 规则黑洞的引力扰动特性与有效势垒高度随变形参数增大而升高的行为一致，偏离Schwarzschild极限时环荡振荡更快，阻尼行为呈现非单调变化。

Abstract: We study axial gravitational perturbations of the neutral regular black hole generated by a non-local, T-duality-inspired zero-point length and the associated gravitational self-energy. In this geometry, the usual point source is replaced by a regular core, and the zero-point length controls the departure from the Schwarzschild limit. We compute the fundamental quasinormal modes and several overtones using high-order WKB--Padé methods, and we verify the dominant mode via direct time-domain evolution. When the zero-point length is turned on, the real parts of the ADM-scaled frequencies increase for the gravitational modes with $\ell=2,3,4$, so the ringdown oscillates faster than in the Schwarzschild limit. The damping rates change more gradually: they initially increase slightly and then decrease near the largest deformation values considered here. This behavior is consistent with the effective potential, whose barrier becomes higher as the deformation parameter increases. We also compute the corresponding excitation factors and find that their magnitudes vary much less strongly than the quasinormal frequencies.

</details>


### [138] [Deformed Compact Objects in General Relativity and Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07736)
*Jonathan Tejeda Quartuccio*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文研究了中子星等致密天体在不同引力理论（广义相对论、D-TOV变形方案、f(R,T)引力）下的流体静力学平衡，发现形状变形参数和物质-几何耦合显著影响恒星的质量-半径关系和最大质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密天体（中子星等）是研究超核密度物质和强场引力的独特实验室，需探索不同几何与引力框架下致密星的静力学平衡特性。

Method: 推导了GR中的标准TOV方程，引入单参数有效变形方案得到D-TOV形式，并将静力学平衡框架扩展到f(R,T)引力；使用GM1状态方程、MIT袋模型及多方状态方程计算平衡序列。

Result: 在GR中，变形参数D显著影响恒星性质：扁球构型支持更大的质量和半径，长球构型导致质量更小、更致密；在优化的线性和f(R,T)模型中，含迹的物质-几何耦合进一步修正质量-半径关系和最大质量。

Conclusion: 有效变形和含迹的物质-几何耦合能显著影响致密星的平衡结构。

Abstract: Neutron stars and related compact objects are unique laboratories for probing matter at supranuclear densities and gravity in the strong-field regime. In this thesis, we investigate the hydrostatic equilibrium of compact stars in different geometric and gravitational scenarios, combining relativistic stellar structure, dense-matter microphysics, and numerical modeling. We derive the standard Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equation in General Relativity, introduce an effective one-parameter deformation scheme leading to the deformed TOV (D-TOV) formalism, and extend the hydrostatic equilibrium framework to f(R,T) gravity.
  Using these structure equations, we compute equilibrium sequences for neutron stars and strange stars described by the GM1 equation of state, the MIT Bag Model, and a polytropic equation of state. In General Relativity, the deformation parameter D significantly affects the global stellar properties: oblate configurations generally support larger masses and radii than the spherical case, while prolate configurations lead to less massive and more compact stars. In optimized and linear f(R,T) models, trace-dependent matter-geometry couplings further modify the mass-radius relation and the maximum supported mass. Overall, the results indicate that effective deformation and trace-dependent matter-geometry coupling can significantly affect the equilibrium structure of compact stars.

</details>


### [139] [Systematic Light Propagation Bias from the Heliosphere and Its Impact on the Hubble Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07741)
*Behnam Pourhassan,İzzet Sakallı,Sudhaker Upadhyay,Kairat Myrzakulov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出哈勃张力（本地测量H₀=73.5 vs CMB预测H₀=67.4 km/s/Mpc）可能源于太阳系日球层环境对本地距离测量的系统误差，日球层产生的等离子体和能量中性原子环境可能影响造父变星和Ia型超新星观测，导致哈勃常数被高估，理论计算显示该效应可解释3-8%的差异（总差异8-9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前哈勃常数本地测量与CMB预测之间存在8-9%的显著差异（哈勃张力），传统解释倾向于新物理，但本文探索是否有系统误差来源，特别是太阳系日球层环境对天文观测的影响是否被忽视。

Method: 理论分析日球层结构（太阳风、等离子体、能量中性原子）对局域距离阶梯观测的影响机制，包括热梯度效应、粒子环境对测光和光谱的影响，并考虑各向异性、部分校准抵消和色散抑制等因素后计算定量贡献。

Result: 日球层效应理论上可解释哈勃张力中约3-8%的差异（占8-9%总差异的比例），最乐观情况下可达~8%，但实际贡献可能更低。总差异约8-9%，而该效应可贡献3-8%，说明仍有剩余差异需要其他机制解释。

Conclusion: 日球层环境可能是哈勃张力的重要系统误差来源之一，但不能完全解释全部差异；需要进一步的观测检验来验证该假设，并精确量化其贡献。

Abstract: The Hubble tension (HT) represents one of the most significant discrepancies in modern cosmology, with local distance measurements yielding $H_0 = 73.5 \pm 1.4$ km/s/Mpc while cosmic microwave background (CMB) observations predict $H_0 = 67.4 \pm 0.5$ km/s/Mpc. We propose that this tension arises from systematic effects introduced by our Solar System's heliospheric (HS) environment on local distance measurements. The HS creates a complex medium of heated plasma and energetic neutral atoms (ENAs) beyond the heliopause (HP), where interstellar medium (ISM) temperatures rise significantly. This thermal gradient and particle environment may systematically affect observations of Cepheid variables and Type Ia supernovae (SNe~Ia) used in the local cosmic distance ladder (CDL), biasing distance measurements and artificially inflating the measured Hubble constant. We present theoretical calculations showing how HS effects could account for up to $\sim 8\%$ of the observed 8--9\% discrepancy, with the realistic contribution lying in the $\sim 3$--$8\%$ range once anisotropy, partial calibration cancellation, and chromatic suppression are included, and discuss observational tests to validate this hypothesis.

</details>


### [140] [Observational Limits on Einasto Dark Matter Parameters from Event Horizon Telescope Images of Sgr A$^{*}$ and M87$^{*}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07752)
*A. Errehymy,S. Hansraj,C. Hansraj*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用事件视界望远镜（EHT）对Sgr A*和M87*的阴影观测数据，结合Einasto暗物质晕模型，通过贝叶斯参数估计对黑洞周围暗物质分布施加约束，发现Sgr A*附近的暗物质密度上限约为10^{-11} M⊙/pc³（1σ置信度），而M87*的约束较弱，主要限制的是光子球附近的质量而非剖面斜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHT提供了对超大质量黑洞Sgr A*和M87*的直接成像，使得通过黑洞阴影测量来探测暗物质晕内部结构成为可能。任何扩展质量分布（如暗物质晕）都会扰动光子环区域的零测地线，因此阴影测量可以作为内晕结构的探针。本文旨在利用EHT阴影数据对Einasto型暗物质晕参数空间进行约束。

Method: 从Einasto密度剖面出发，构建度规函数f(r)=1-2M/r+2M_∞ g̃(r)，该函数在黑洞视界与渐近晕之间插值。分析光子势、零测地线和阴影半径作为黑洞质量M的函数（非自旋极限）。利用EHT测得的无量纲阴影直径d_sh进行贝叶斯参数估计，识别Einasto参数空间的允许区域。

Result: 对Sgr A*，采用恒星轨道质量先验，在1σ置信度下得到ρ₀ ≲ 10^{-11} M⊙/pc³；对M87*，由于距离不确定性，约束较弱。Einasto指数ν̃约束较弱，表明EHT精度主要限制的是光子球附近的质量而非剖面斜率。

Conclusion: EHT阴影数据能有效约束黑洞周围暗物质晕的内部密度，但当前精度主要限制质量而非剖面形状。未来EHT观测将精化这些约束，并区分不同暗物质模型。

Abstract: The Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) has provided images of the supermassive black-holes Sgr A$^{*}$ and M87$^{*}$, enabling direct tests of gravity. Any extended mass-distribution, such as a dark-matter halo, perturbs null-geodesics in the photon-ring regime, making shadow-measurements a probe of inner-halo structure. In this work, we investigate static, spherically-symmetric black-holes surrounded by Einasto-type dark-matter halos and derive constraints from EHT shadow-data. Starting from the Einasto density-profile with parameters ${\varrho_0, \tildeα, \tildeν}$, we construct a metric-function $f(r)=1-2M/r+2M_\infty \tilde{g}(r)$ that interpolates between the black-hole horizon and the asymptotic halo, following the approach of Xu et al. (2018) but adapted specifically to the Einasto scenario. We analyze the photon-potential, null-geodesics, and shadow-radius as functions of black-hole mass $M$, in the non-spinning limit. Using the dimensionless shadow-diameter $d_{\text{sh}}\equiv Dθ/M$ measured by the EHT -- $d_{\text{sh}}^{M87*}=11.0\pm1.5$ and $d_{\text{sh}}^{SgrA*}=9.5\pm1.4$ -- we perform Bayesian parameter-estimation to identify allowed regions in the Einasto parameter-space. Combined with the independently-measured black-hole masses from stellar-dynamics, our results place constraints on the inner dark-matter distribution: for Sgr A$^{*}$, adopting the stellar-orbit mass-prior, we find $\varrho_0 \lesssim 10^{-11},M_\odot/\text{pc}^3$ at $1σ$ confidence, while for M87$^{*}$ the bounds are weaker due to distance-uncertainties. The Einasto-index $\tildeν$ is weakly-constrained, indicating that EHT precision primarily limits the mass enclosed near the photon-sphere rather than the profile-slope. Future EHT observations will refine these constraints and distinguish between competing dark-matter descriptions.

</details>


### [141] [Regular Black Holes in Nonlocal Quasitopological Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07790)
*Pablo Bueno,Pablo A. Cano,Robie A. Hennigar,Ángel J. Murcia*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了无限导数版本的拟拓扑引力理论，该理论无鬼场、避免强耦合不稳定性，并允许精确的球对称真空正则黑洞解，满足微扰Birkhoff定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决拟拓扑引力中存在的鬼场和强耦合不稳定性问题，同时构造正则（无奇点）黑洞解。

Method: 通过引入无限导数（非局域）算子对拟拓扑引力进行完备化，构建ghost-free的高阶导数引力理论。

Result: 得到了精确的球对称真空正则黑洞解，且该解满足微扰Birkhoff定理，即球对称扰动下解的唯一性得以保持。

Conclusion: 无限导数完备化是一种有效消除拟拓扑引力中病理行为（鬼场、强耦合）并允许正则黑洞解存在的方案。

Abstract: We present infinite-derivative completions of Quasitopological gravities that are ghost-free, avoid strong coupling instabilities and admit exact, spherically symmetric vacuum regular-black-hole solutions satisfying a perturbative Birkhoff theorem.

</details>


### [142] [Singularity resolution and unitarity in two-dimensional dilaton black holes with negative central charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07806)
*César García-Pérez,F. Javier Marañón-González,José Navarro-Salas,Silvia Pla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类一阶量子修正的CGHS二维引力模型，通过有效的非局域作用量，在负总中心荷区域消除了经典曲率奇点，获得了视界内部渐近平坦区域，并展示了与外部霍金辐射相关联的内部辐射通量（含短暂负值），指向幺正性保存的可能性，但完全一致的能量平衡尚未建立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展经典CGHS二维引力模型，引入一阶量子修正，以探索黑洞信息丢失问题及量子引力中奇点消除的可能性。

Method: 构建包含非局域Polyakov作用量（物质涨落）、基于辅助平坦度规的Polyakov型项（Strominger机制处理重参数化鬼场）以及局域反项（保持辅助度规平坦、确保精确可解性和闵氏时空解）的有效作用量。

Result: 负总中心荷下，经典曲率奇点被消除，视界内部出现渐近平坦区域；外部霍金通量得以保持，并与来自视界内部的辐射通量相关，该内部通量存在短暂负值区间；相关性暗示幺正性可能得以保存。

Conclusion: 该模型展现了从量子修正中消除奇点和保存幺正性的前景，但当前形式下能量一致性尚未完全满足，需要进一步探索改进策略。

Abstract: We study a one-loop corrected extension of the classical Callan-Giddings-Harvey-Strominger (CGHS) model of two-dimensional dilaton gravity. The effective action combines the non-local Polyakov action for matter fluctuations, a Polyakov-type term built from an auxiliary flat metric that implements Strominger's mechanism for the Faddeev-Popov reparametrization ghosts, and a local counterterm that simultaneously preserves the flatness of the auxiliary metric, ensures exact solvability, and keeps two-dimensional Minkowski spacetime as an exact solution of the backreacted equations. In the regime of negative total central charge, the classical curvature singularity is resolved and gives way to asymptotically flat regions inside the horizon. The exterior Hawking flux is preserved and turns out to be correlated with an internal radiation flux supported on null surfaces that approach null infinity from beyond the horizon; this internal flux, in particular, presents a short interval of negative values. These correlations point to the preservation of unitarity, provided the relevant null surfaces remain at a finite affine distance from the collapsing matter trajectory. Within the present formulation, however, a fully consistent energy balance cannot yet be established. We discuss possible strategies to overcome this issue.

</details>


### [143] [Causal Horizons, Geodesic Completeness and Stability in Slow Contraction Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07815)
*Mariam Khaldieh,Anna I. Rosenzweig,Paul J. Steinhardt*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文论证了包含半无限慢收缩（ekpyrosis）阶段的宇宙学模型具有独特性质组合：渐近闵可夫斯基空间的过去稳定吸引子、平坦均匀各向同性的未来吸引子、无粒子视界、过去测地线完备性，从而解决了标准暴涨或德西特收缩模型中的因果视界问题、BGV定理奇点问题及引力熵问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准宇宙学模型中慢膨胀（暴涨）或德西特收缩阶段面临的基本问题，包括粒子视界问题、过去测地线不完备性（BGV定理）、引力熵问题以及收缩相的不稳定性。

Method: 通过理论分析研究慢收缩（ekpyrosis）宇宙学模型的吸引子性质、因果结构、测地线完备性以及量子涨落谱，并与德西特收缩和标准大爆炸膨胀模型进行对比。

Result: 慢收缩模型具有：1）渐近闵可夫斯基空间的稳定过去吸引子；2）平坦、均匀、各向同性且Weyl曲率可忽略的稳定未来吸引子；3）无粒子视界；4）沿过去向测地线的平均膨胀率非正，因此测地线完备且规避BGV定理；5）产生全波长的真空类量子涨落初始谱。相比之下，收缩德西特空间存在有限粒子视界且在标量场、物质或辐射存在下不稳定。

Conclusion: 包含半无限慢收缩阶段的宇宙学模型能够同时解决因果视界问题、过去奇点问题（BGV定理）和引力熵问题，为反弹宇宙学提供了优越的初始条件设置机制。

Abstract: We show that cosmological models with a semi-infinite phase of slow contraction (ekpyrosis) possess a combination of properties that can address several fundamental problems in cosmology, otherwise faced in contracting de Sitter phases or standard big bang expansion. In particular, slow contraction admits a stable past attractor asymptoting to Minkowski space, as well as a stable, flat, homogeneous, and isotropic future attractor with negligible Weyl curvature (and, therefore, negligible gravitational entropy). In bouncing cosmologies, this contracting attractor is terminated by a smooth, non-singular bounce that transforms the attractor properties at the end of contraction into the initial conditions for the subsequent expanding phase. Cosmologies incorporating a slow contraction phase have no particle horizon and therefore avoid the causal horizon problem. The past Minkowski attractor also generates an initial spectrum of vacuum-like quantum fluctuations on all wavelengths. Moreover, because the averaged expansion rate along past-directed geodesics is non-positive, models incorporating a semi-infinite phase of slow contraction also evade the Borde,Guth and Vilenkin theorem and are past geodesically complete. By contrast, contracting de Sitter space possesses a finite particle horizon and becomes unstable in the presence of scalar fields, matter, or radiation.

</details>


### [144] [Charging up regular black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07831)
*Raúl Carballo-Rubio,Chiara Coviello,Vania Vellucci*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在球对称广义爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦场方程框架下构造带电正则黑洞的一般性方法，发现Bardeen和Hayward黑洞在带电情况下会变得奇异，并提出了改进版本以保证正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正则黑洞模型（如Bardeen和Hayward黑洞）通常在真空无电荷情况下构造，但其带电版本的正则性尚未得到充分验证。作者希望建立一般性框架来研究带电正则黑洞的条件，并指出单纯将真空正则解推广到带电情形并不总能保持正则性。

Method: 推广爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦方程组，引入变形爱因斯坦张量（包含最高二阶导数的守恒张量），在球对称假设下系统分析带电正则黑洞所需满足的边界条件与正则性判据，并对比带电与不带电情形的差异。

Result: 带电正则黑洞的正则性条件比不带电情形更严格；Bardeen和Hayward黑洞在带电时出现奇点；作者提出了改进的Bardeen和Hayward度规，使其在带电时仍保持正则。

Conclusion: 真空解的正则化不足以保证其他物理情景（如带电、旋转、存在物质场）下的正则性，构造实际物理正则黑洞需要考虑更多因素。

Abstract: We present a general construction of charged regular black holes as solutions of a generalization of the Einstein--Maxwell field equations in spherical symmetry in which the Einstein tensor is deformed into an identically conserved tensor containing up to second derivatives of the gravitational field. The generality of the construction allows us to define the field equations satisfied by generic regular black holes when becoming charged. The conditions that guarantee regularity of charged solutions are evaluated and shown to be more stringent than the regularity conditions for uncharged solutions. This implies, in particular, that the charged versions of the Bardeen and Hayward black holes become singular. Improved versions of the Bardeen and Hayward metrics that remain regular when charged are proposed. Our results indicate that regularizing the vacuum solutions of general relativity is, in general, not enough to yield regular solutions in other situations of physical interest. The implications that follow for the construction of realistic regular black holes, in which aspects such as rotation and the presence of matter fields are taken into account, are discussed.

</details>


### [145] [Finite mass two throat wormholes: global light rings, branch resolved strong lensing, and scalar transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07839)
*Anirudh Pradhan,K. Ghaderi,M. Zeyauddin,K. Karimizadeh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个静态、无视界、渐近平直的双喉径虫洞族，其中单一全局度量决定了几何、有效源、零动力学、透镜效应和测试场传播。通过有限质量形变四次嵌入轮廓产生两个对称喉径和中间赤道，Misner-Sharp质量在两渐近端趋于相同有限值。获得了完整的径向平均零泛函并严格为负。全局零势允许对所有正质量和非负红移形变进行完整相分类。推导了对数强偏转系数的解析表达式，并验证了跨喉系数是射线穿越的每个全局最大值的贡献之和。标量散射问题表现出相位相关势垒和共振透射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个既包含有限质量又不含视界的虫洞模型，使得拓扑、平均能量条件、分支解析的强透镜效应和波传输能够从单一时空推导出来，为虫洞物理提供更完整的理论框架。

Method: 采用有限质量形变的四次嵌入轮廓生成对称双喉径虫洞，通过全局度量统一描述几何和动力学性质；解析推导径向平均零泛函、全局零势的相分类、对数强偏转系数等，并用直接数值积分验证。

Result: 获得了严格为负的完整径向平均零泛函；根据参数不同，全局不稳定集由喉径环、四喉径外环或外环伴随亚临界赤道环组成；在四环相中所有不稳定环具有相同冲击尺度但内外分支Lyapunov指数不同；跨喉偏转系数等于射线穿越所有全局最大值的贡献之和。

Conclusion: 该模型在有限质量框架下，从单一时空几何统一了虫洞拓扑、平均能量条件、分支解析的强透镜和标量波传输现象，为虫洞理论提供了自洽的解析基础。

Abstract: We introduce a static, horizonless, asymptotically flat two throat wormhole family in which one global metric determines the geometry, effective source, null dynamics, lensing, and test field propagation. A finite mass deformation of a quartic embedding profile produces two symmetric throats and an intermediate equator, while the associated Misner--Sharp mass tends to the same finite value at both asymptotic ends. The temporal sector yields the same mass parameter, and the Einstein tensor defines a single conserved anisotropic source. In addition to the local flare out identity, the complete radial averaged null functional is obtained exactly and is strictly negative. The global null potential admits a complete phase classification for all positive masses and nonnegative redshift deformation. Depending on the parameters, the global unstable set consists of throat rings, four off throat rings, or exterior rings accompanied by a subcritical equatorial ring. In the four ring phase, all unstable rings have the same impact scale but the inner and outer branches have different Lyapunov exponents. We derive the logarithmic strong deflection coefficients analytically and show that the cross throat coefficient is the sum of the contributions from every global maximum traversed by the ray. Direct numerical integration verifies these coefficients. The scalar scattering problem exhibits phase dependent barriers and resonant transmission. The resulting model provides a finite mass setting in which topology, averaged energy conditions, branch resolved strong lensing, and wave transmission are derived from one spacetime.

</details>


### [146] [Nonlinear hydrodynamics in spinning neutron stars: Theoretical universal relations and equilibrium solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07943)
*Hang Yu,Giorgio Nicolini,Shu Yan Lau,K. J. Kwon,Tejaswi Venumadhav,Nils Andersson,Pantelis Pnigouras,Fabian Gittins,Amlan Nanda*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了双中子星并合过程中非线性流体动力学潮汐相互作用，利用仿射近似解析推导了f模和径向模到四波阶的耦合系数，发现三波非线性潮汐的相互作用系数完全由线性潮汐性质决定，不会带来新物理，但忽略它们会导致引力波形的显著系统误差；单个中子星的非线性潮汐相比线性模型可累积约1.7弧度的相移，双星系统约翻倍；快速旋转中子星的四波非简谐性可提供研究内部浮力绝热指数的窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双中子星并合过程中的非线性潮汐相互作用对引力波波形有重要影响，现有理论多限于线性潮汐或仅考虑三波相互作用，缺乏对更高阶非线性效应的系统理解和解析推导。

Method: 采用仿射近似将受扰中子星视为椭球体，从哈密顿量解析推导到四波阶（次次领头阶）的f模与径向模耦合系数；结合牛顿流体动力学中的模共振和相对论低频展开的混合方法来评估波形相位误差。

Result: 三波相互作用系数完全由线性潮汐决定，不提供新物理；但忽略三波非线性潮汐会导致引力波相位误差显著；单个中子星非线性潮汐引起~1.7 rad相移，双星约翻倍；四波相互作用在慢速旋转时贡献很小；快速旋转时f模的非线性离心驱动和四波非简谐性可探测绝热指数。

Conclusion: 非线性潮汐效应在双中子星引力波波形建模中不可忽略，尤其是三波非线性潮汐；快速旋转的中子星为研究内部结构提供了新的探针，但f模不会被四波非简谐性锁定在共振中。

Abstract: We study tides during the inspiral of a binary neutron star (BNS) system, including nonlinear hydrodynamical interactions. Using an affine approximation that treats the perturbed NS as an ellipsoid, we analytically derive coupling coefficients among the f-modes and the radial mode to the four-wave order (i.e., next-to-next-to-leading order) in the Hamiltonian, allowing for arbitrary rotation of the background star. Our model reveals a series of universal relations from first-principles arguments. Besides the well-known relations, we show that the three-wave (next-to-leading-order) interaction coefficients are fully determined by the properties of the linear tide. Therefore, they do not probe new physics of the NS. Nonetheless, not including the three-wave nonlinear tides can lead to significant systematic errors in the gravitational waveform. We support this claim via a hybrid approach that simultaneously captures mode resonances expected in Newtonian hydrodynamics and is consistent with relativistic calculations in the low-frequency expansion. The nonlinear tide in a single NS can cause a phase shift of around 1.7 radians accumulated up to merger compared to the linear tide model; for a binary of similar masses, the phase shift is approximately doubled. Our calculation extends to four-wave interactions, which, for a slowly spinning NS, provide only small corrections and are subdominant compared to the tidal back-reaction on the orbit. For a rapidly rotating NS, the nonlinear centrifugal drive of the f-mode and the four-wave anharmonicity provides a window to study the adiabatic exponent related to internal buoyancy that cannot be probed by the linear and three-wave tides in slowly spinning systems. The anharmonicity cannot lead to resonance locking of the f-mode.

</details>


### [147] [Wave and particle probes of a regular T-duality-inspired black hole with gravitational self-energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07955)
*Alexey Dubinsky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种受非局域T-对偶性启发的点质量涂抹方法，该方法引入了有限零-point长度，使牛顿引力自能正则化并成为时空的额外源，产生非奇异黑洞几何。研究表明，零-point长度平滑了中心区域并改变了视界外的强场势，影响标量场 ringing 和粒子/光运动：更重的标量场导致更高振荡频率和更弱阻尼（长寿命ringing），更大的零-point长度使光子轨道和ISCO更致密、阴影更小、光子环频率更大、轨道结合能增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索T-对偶性启发的非局域点质量涂抹方法引入的零-point长度如何影响黑洞物理，特别是标量场ringing信号和粒子/光在视界附近的运动，以理解这种正则化机制的可观测效应。

Method: 通过理论分析研究零-point长度对标量场ringing和粒子/光轨道的影响，比较不同零-point长度和标量场质量下的物理行为变化。

Result: 1) 更重的标量场增加振荡频率并减弱阻尼（长寿命ringing）；2) 更大的零-point长度使光子轨道和ISCO更致密、阴影更小、光子环频率更大、轨道结合能增加。

Conclusion: 零-point长度的正则化校正会在波传播、黑洞阴影和圆轨道物理中留下相关的可观测印记。

Abstract: Recently it was shown that a non-local T-duality-inspired smearing of the point mass introduces a finite zero-point length, while the regularized Newtonian gravitational self-energy is promoted to an additional source for the spacetime. The result is a nonsingular black-hole geometry whose ADM mass contains a finite self-energy contribution and whose extremal Planck-scale remnant sector has been proposed as a possible dark-matter component. We study how this spacetime would affect two familiar physical signals: the ringing of a massive scalar field and the motion of particles and light near the horizon. The zero-point length smooths the central region and changes the strong-field potential outside the horizon. On the wave side, we find that making the scalar field heavier increases the oscillation frequency and makes the damping weaker, a behavior associated with long-lived ringing. On the particle side, increasing the zero-point length makes the photon orbit and the innermost stable circular orbit more compact in physical mass units. The corresponding shadow becomes smaller, the photon-ring frequency becomes larger, and the orbital binding energy increases. These results show that the same regularizing correction leaves related imprints in wave propagation, black-hole shadows and circular-orbit physics.

</details>


### [148] [Acceleration in 3D Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08030)
*Cendikiawan Suryaatmadja,Dong-han Yeom,Robert Mann*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力理论中，在2+1维度上发现了一类新的精确解，推广了C-metric，并发现了六个不同的子类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在2+1维的Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力中寻找更丰富的精确解结构，超越了标准Einstein引力中的C-metric解。

Method: 通过引入一个质量less标量场，将C-metric乘以一个依赖于单一变量的因子，构造出新的度规族，并对解的结构进行分类和分析。

Result: 在Einstein引力的三个度规类中，每类都发现了六个不同的子类；其中一个子类是局部AdS的，在零耦合极限下不仅恢复C-metric，还发现两个新的AdS时空表示；此外还发现了非恒定曲率的新解和畴壁的存在性。

Conclusion: Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力在2+1维中比标准Einstein引力具有更丰富的解结构，某些子类具有清晰的物理解释（如AdS和畴壁），而另一些非恒定曲率解的解释尚待进一步研究。

Abstract: We present a new class of exact solutions in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity in 2+1 dimensions that generalize the C-metric. This set of metrics equals the C-metric multiplied by a factor which, along with the massless scalar field, depends on a single variable whose value governs the structure of the spacetime. As in Einstein gravity there are three classes of metrics, but within each class we find six distinct subclasses of solutions. After discussing their basic structure, we concentrate only on one subclass that is locally AdS. In the zero-coupling limit, this subclass of solutions not only remains well defined and recovers the C-metric but also encompasses two previously unknown representations of the AdS spacetime. Furthermore, we establish the existence of a domain wall and delineate its energy conditions. We also find new classes of solutions of non-constant curvature, whose interpretation remains to be understood.

</details>


### [149] [An analytic model for a total process of gravitational collapse: From star to Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08088)
*Sinya Aoki,Jorge Ovalle*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个精确解析模型，完整描述了物质从无视界初始条件到黑洞形成的引力坍缩过程，追踪了视界从微观尺度形成到宏观稳定的完整演化。模型揭示了两个主要阶段：动力视界增长（在临界时刻出现表观视界并最终达到稳定大小）和裸奇点解决（可积的曲率奇点被视界增长因果性地隐藏，无需奇异物质即可保护弱宇宙监督假设）。该模型为研究量子-经典过渡提供了框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引力坍缩理论缺乏完整的解析模型来描述视界从形成到稳定的全过程，尤其是在有限时间内俘获面的动态形成机制以及裸奇点问题的因果关系尚不清楚。

Method: 通过精确解析建模，从无视界的初始条件出发，求解引力坍缩方程，得到视界H(t)从微观尺度（普朗克尺度）到宏观稳定尺度（h=2M）的完整演化轨迹。

Result: 模型发现两个关键阶段：(1) 在临界时间t_b出现表观视界并动态增长至最终尺寸h=2M；(2) 在r=0处出现可积的里奇曲率奇点（R^μ_ν ~ r^{-2}），但该奇点被增长的视界因果性地隐藏，无需外来物质即可满足弱宇宙监督假设。

Conclusion: 该模型为理解黑洞形成中的量子-经典过渡（视界在普朗克尺度附近的行为）提供了理论框架，并表明无需引入奇异物质即可在广义相对论框架内保护弱宇宙监督假设。

Abstract: We present an exact analytical model describing a complete gravitational collapse of matter from horizonless initial conditions to black hole formation, tracing the full evolution of the horizon $H(t)$ from its formation at microscopic scales to macroscopic stabilization. The solution reveals two main stages: (i) a dynamical horizon growth, where an apparent horizon $H(t)$ emerges at a critical time $t_b$ until reaching its final size $h=2 M$, demonstrating how trapped surfaces form dynamically in finite time, and (ii) a {naked-}singularity resolution, where an integrable Ricci curvature singularity ($R^μ_ν\sim r^{-2}$) develops at $r=0$, but remains causally hidden by the horizon growth, preserving weak cosmic censorship without exotic matter. The model could offer a framework to study the quantum-to-classical transition ($H(t) \sim \ell_{\rm Planck}$).

</details>


### [150] [Field Theoretic Aspects of Gravity From Direct Action to Quantum Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08134)
*Ghanashyam Date*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文比较了引力相互作用的直接作用观点（基于马赫原理）与场论观点，并追踪了从经典到量子框架下场概念的演变，特别关注量子场论中引力带来的新特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨"超距作用"引力观（受马赫原理驱动）与场论观点之间的根本差异，以及这种差异在量子框架下的演变。

Method: 从经典框架下直接作用观与场论观的比较出发，过渡到量子框架，梳理量子场论的发展历程及引力引入后的新特征。

Result: 在经典框架中，直接作用观排斥场作为力的中介；但在量子框架中，场的概念从力的中介升格为自主的物理实体。

Conclusion: 量子场论中场的角色发生了根本性转变，引力进一步丰富了量子场论的图景，但本文仅为概念性梳理而非系统综述。

Abstract: One of the technical anchors of JVN's research was the "action-at-a-distance" view of gravitational interaction, driven by "Mach's Principle" addressing the origin of inertia. This view exorcises the concept of a field as a mediator of force between separated "particles". However, within a quantum framework, the concept of field as a mediator transcends to field as an autonomous physical entity. Beginning with a comparison between direct action view and field theoretic view in classical framework and moving over to quantum framework, I sketch the strides made in quantum field theory including the new features brought in by gravity. This is intended as a conceptual trace rather than a review.

</details>


### [151] [Spectroscopic structure of Non-Hermitian $\mathcal{PT}$-Symmetric Klein--Gordon Fields in a Magnetized Cosmic String Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08148)
*Omar Mustafa,Abdullah Guvendi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了磁化宇宙弦时空中的非厄米$\mathcal{PT}$对称Klein-Gordon场，通过引入复数非最小标量相互作用，构建了$\mathcal{PT}$对称Klein-Gordon振子模型，并利用双合流Heun级数-多项式方法实现了条件精确可解。与厄米情形相比，$\mathcal{PT}$对称化导致允许能谱出现上限，为弯曲时空中非厄米相对论量子场提供了可解析处理的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索弯曲时空中非厄米$\mathcal{PT}$对称相对论量子场的可解模型，并研究$\mathcal{PT}$对称性对能谱的调控作用。

Method: 采用复数非最小标量相互作用$\mathcal{F}_\mu=(0,\mathcal{F}_r,0,0)$其中$\mathcal{F}_r=\omega r+ib\in\mathbb{C}$，并引入磁荷$q=ie$，将系统转化为$\mathcal{PT}$对称Klein-Gordon振子模型。径向方程利用双合流Heun级数-多项式方法实现条件精确可解。通过映射$e=-i\tilde e$和$b=-i\tilde b$与厄米情形对比。极限$\omega=0$时，退化为可用合流超几何级数/多项式精确求解的$\mathcal{PT}$对称Coulomb型KG方程。

Result: 对于所考虑的两种$\mathcal{PT}$对称模型，非厄米$\mathcal{PT}$对称化引入了允许能量的上限，这与对应的厄米情形不同。磁化宇宙弦时空中的$\mathcal{PT}$对称Klein-Gordon系统具有条件精确可解性。

Conclusion: 该研究为探索弯曲时空中非厄米相对论量子场提供了一个可解析处理的框架，并展示了$\mathcal{PT}$对称性在调控允许能谱中的作用。

Abstract: We investigate non-Hermitian $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric Klein--Gordon (KG) fields in a magnetized cosmic string spacetime. A complex non-minimal scalar interaction, $\mathcal{F}_μ=(0,\mathcal{F}_{r},0,0)$ with $\mathcal{F}_{r}=ωr+ib\in\mathbb{C}$, indulged with a magnetic charge $q=ie$, is shown to introduce an effective $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric Klein--Gordon oscillator supplemented by complex Coulombic and linear interactions. The resulting radial equation is shown to be conditionally exactly solvable using a biconfluent Heun series-to-polynomial approach. To observe non-Hermitian \(\mathcal{PT}\) symmetrization, we compare with the Hermitian counterpart by mapping $e=-i\tilde e \Rightarrow q\in \mathbb R$ and $b=-i\tilde b \Rightarrow \mathcal F_r\in \mathbb R$. In the limiting case $ω=0$, the system is shown to reduce to an exactly solvable non-Hermitian \(\mathcal{PT}\) symmetric Coulomb-type KG equation using confluent hypergeometric series/polynomials. Hereby, for both \(\mathcal{PT}\) symmetric models considered, we show that non-Hermitian \(\mathcal{PT}\) symmetrization introduces an upper limit for the allowed energies, unlike the corresponding Hermitian cases. These results provide an analytically tractable framework for exploring non-Hermitian relativistic quantum fields in curved spacetimes and demonstrate the role of $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetry in regulating the allowed spectrum.

</details>


### [152] [Static regular black holes in Horndeski theories: analytic no-go and nonanalytic obstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08228)
*Antonio De Felice,Shinji Tsujikawa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: Horndeski理论中的正则黑洞必须具有稳定视界和正则中心。研究表明，除非存在特殊退化，否则标量动能项在视界处非零的分支通常被发散传播速度或鬼影/梯度不稳定性所阻碍。在正则分支上，移位对称非简并理论导出非微扰电流无毛定理：标量为常数，度规为Schwarzschild，非零ADM质量下中心奇点不可避免。非移位对称正幂耦合则排除与Schwarzschild连续连接的微扰分支。唯一边际非解析完备是标量-Gauss-Bonnet链，但该完备中的毛状黑洞仍保留中心奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Horndeski理论中正则黑洞的存在条件，特别是标量场非平凡构型能否同时满足视界稳定性和中心正则性。

Method: 通过分析静态球对称渐近平直时空中标量场方程的解析性，分别研究视界处标量动能项X非零（视界分支）和为零（正则分支）两种情况，并分类边际非解析偏离。

Result: 视界分支普遍存在不稳定性；正则分支中，移位对称理论推出非微扰电流无毛定理，非移位对称理论排除微扰分支；唯一例外是标量-GB链，但其黑洞仍有中心奇点。

Conclusion: 在Horndeski理论框架下，同时满足视界稳定和中心正则的黑洞解几乎不存在，标准Schwarzschild奇点问题未能通过引入标量场得到解决。

Abstract: Regular black holes in Horndeski theories must have stable horizons and regular centers. We study static, spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat configurations with a time-independent scalar. The horizon branch on which the scalar kinetic term $X$ remains nonzero is generically obstructed by divergent propagation speeds or ghost/gradient instabilities, aside from special degeneracies. On the regular branch, where $X$ vanishes at the horizon, analyticity at the relevant $X=0$ endpoints reduces the leading scalar equation to finite sets of Taylor coefficients. For nondegenerate shift-symmetric theories this gives a nonperturbative current no-hair theorem: the scalar is constant and the metric is Schwarzschild, hence centrally singular for nonzero ADM mass. For non-shift-symmetric positive-power couplings, the corresponding exclusion applies to the perturbative branch continuously connected to Schwarzschild. We also classify marginal nonanalytic departures: covariant regularity fixes the scalar-Gauss-Bonnet chain as the unique marginal nonanalytic completion. Hairy black holes in this completion evade the analytic current step but remain centrally singular.

</details>


### [153] [Quasi-bound states and late-time evolution of a massive fermion around a Reissner-Nordström black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08258)
*Guang-Shang Chen,Cheng-Bo Yang,Shou-Shan Bao,Yue-Liang Wu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带电黑洞周围有质量费米子的准束缚态，通过构建格林函数和改进的匹配方案，得到了更精确的能谱和衰变宽度，并分析了晚期时间演化行为，发现了振荡幂律衰减和指数抑制的 chirping 相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典黑洞与量子场论结合可揭示引力系统的类原子束缚态及其弛豫行为，但目前对带电黑洞周围有质量费米子的准束缚态谱，尤其弱耦合情形下的精细结构和衰变宽度，尚缺乏精确解析表达式。

Method: 将径向方程化为耦合矩阵系统，构造满足视界内落边界条件和无穷远衰减边界条件的格林函数；在弱耦合条件下使用矩阵匹配方案推导准束缚态谱的解析表达式；分别处理极端和非极端 Reissner-Nordström 情形；分析时域格林函数的支割线贡献，并直接进行时域数值模拟验证。

Result: 改进了准束缚态谱的解析表达式，包含精细结构修正和更精确的衰变宽度；证明极端 RN 情形是非极端谱的光滑极限；在中等晚期时间区间观测到振荡幂律行为；在更远晚期时间区间发现 t^{-5/6}exp(-ηt^{1/3}) 抑制和 chirping 相位，最终趋于 t^{-5/6} 尾部。

Conclusion: 准束缚态贡献与幂律分量在时域中共存，通过时域模拟验证了不同晚期时间区间中不同衰减行为的过渡和区分。

Abstract: A massive fermion around a charged black hole provides a gravitational analogue of atomic bound states and their relaxation. In this work, we study this system by formulating the radial equation as a coupled matrix system and constructing the Green's function with ingoing boundary conditions at the horizon and decaying boundary conditions at infinity. In the weak-coupling scenario $|qQ|\sim mM<1$, a matrix matching scheme gives an improved analytic expression of quasi-bound-state spectrum, including fine-structure corrections and more accurate decay widths. The extremal Reissner-Nordström case ($|Q|=M$) is treated separately and shown to be the smooth limiting result of the non-extremal spectrum. We further analyze the branch-cut contribution to the time-domain Green's function in the late-time limit. We confirm an oscillatory power-law behavior in intermediate late-time regime $1/m < t < 1/m^3M^2$. In the far late-time regime $t>1/m^3M^2$, the activation of the quasi-bound states produces an $t^{-5/6}\exp(-ηt^{1/3})$ suppression with a chirping phase before the asymptotic $t^{-5/6}$ tail previously found in the limit $t\to\infty$. Direct time-domain simulations support this distinction and show how the quasi-bound contribution coexists with the familiar power-law component.

</details>


### [154] [5d Schwarzschild-Tangherlini spacetime: MST-like formalism for a Reduced Confluent Heun Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08280)
*Donato Bini,Giorgio Di Russo,Veronica Fantini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维Schwarzschild-Tangherlini解的测地线结构和无质量标量微扰，在探极限下给出了两个应用：（1）利用超几何函数重新求和计算了非束缚测地线的散射角，展示了后牛顿和后闵可夫斯基展开；（2）推导了临界圆轨道偏离的Lyapunov指数，与eikonal近似下准正则模的估计相关。同时，将Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi形式主义拓展到d=5情形，验证了重整化角动量参数ν，并通过量子Seiberg-Witten形式主义独立验证。最后计算了圆轨道的能量通量，得到2.5PN阶的后牛顿结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维时空（五维）黑洞的动力学性质，包括测地线结构、散射过程和微扰理论，以加深对高维引力理论和黑洞物理的理解。

Method: 采用探极限近似，利用超几何函数重新求和计算散射角序列；通过MST形式主义的原创拓展求解五维标量微扰径向方程（约化合流Heun方程）；结合量子Seiberg-Witten形式主义独立验证角动量参数；进行后牛顿展开至2.5PN阶计算圆轨道能量通量。

Result: 成功将散射角序列以超几何函数形式重新求和；推导了Lyapunov指数；将MST形式主义拓展至五维，并验证了重整化角动量参数ν与Seiberg-Witten形式主义的一致性；获得了2.5PN阶的能量通量结果。

Conclusion: 高维Schwarzschild-Tangherlini黑洞的测地线和微扰性质可通过解析方法有效求解，MST形式主义可推广至高维，且与量子方法（Seiberg-Witten）结果一致，为高维黑洞物理提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We study the five-dimensional Schwarzschild-Tangherlini solution, with particular attention to its geodesic structure and massless scalar perturbations. In the probe limit, we present two applications. First, we compute the scattering angle for unbound geodesics showing both post-Newtonian and post-Minkowskian type expansions, and succeeding in resumming the resulting series in terms of hypergeometric functions. Second, we derive the Lyapunov exponent for deviations from a critical circular orbit, which is relevant to the eikonal estimation of quasinormal modes.
  We then investigate the dynamics of massless scalar $(s=0)$ perturbations, for which the radial equation becomes a Reduced Confluent Heun equation. In this $d=5$ Schwarzschild-Tangherlini case we develop an original extension of the standard Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi (MST) formalism and validate the construction by computing the renormalized angular-momentum parameter $ν$, whose value agrees with an independent determination based on the quantum Seiberg-Witten formalism. Finally, we analyze the energy flux from circular orbits, obtaining post-Newtonian results through 2.5PN order.

</details>


### [155] [Chaplygin and Polytropic Kantowski--Sachs Solutions in Teleparallel $F(T)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08343)
*Alexandre Landry*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在teleparallel F(T)引力框架下，基于余标架-自旋联络形式与Coley-Landry不变量方法，针对Kantowski-Sachs几何构建了一个协变重构体系。通过引入广义Chaplygin气体和多方流体等非线性物态方程，利用守恒律确定流体密度与各向异性体积的关系，进而从场方程对称部分重构出F(T)的函数形式。幂律和指数型Ansätze产生不同的重构分支，后者引入移位的de Sitter分支。重构模型被解释为局部各向异性宇宙学扇区或KS黑洞内部扇区，稳定性通过F_T>0和F_{TT}>0初步评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有F(T)引力重构研究多依赖于预先指定拉格朗日量的正向方法，缺乏从非线性物质守恒律出发、反向由动力学驱动的系统性协变重构方案。本文旨在为KS各向异性几何提供一种不依赖于先验引力拉格朗日假设的重构框架，填补teleparallel理论中非线性物质驱动重构的空白。

Method: 采用余标架-自旋联络形式与Coley-Landry不变量方法处理KS几何的协变描述；引入广义Chaplygin气体(p=-A/ρ^α)和多方流体(p=Kρ^Γ)两类非线性物态方程；由守恒律导出密度与KS体积V=A₂A₃²的函数关系；代入teleparallel场方程对称部分，直接求解F(T)的函数形式；考虑幂律和指数型两类Ansätze进行重构。

Result: 幂律扇区中，Chaplygin流体产生常数加幂次的混合源项，多方流体产生受多方指数控制的密度幂次项；指数扇区中，自然重构变量为移位不变量X=T₀-T，产生移位的teleparallel de Sitter分支。重构模型可解释为局部各向异性宇宙学扇区，或在收缩角向尺度因子情形下解释为KS黑洞内部重构分支。通过F_T>0和F_{TT}>0条件初步评估了领头阶可行性。

Conclusion: 成功建立了由非线性物质守恒律反向驱动的F(T)协变重构框架，逆转了传统先验指定引力拉格朗日的策略。重构模型为局部有效，依赖具体分支选择，其完全扰动稳定性需进一步研究。该框架为各向异性几何中teleparallel引力的物质驱动重构提供了系统方法。

Abstract: A covariant reconstruction framework for Kantowski--Sachs (KS) geometries sourced by Chaplygin-type and polytropic fluids in teleparallel $F(T)$ gravity is developed using the coframe--spin-connection formalism and the invariant Coley--Landry approach. The matter sector is modeled by nonlinear equations of state, including the generalized Chaplygin gas $p=-A/ρ^α$ and a polytropic law $p=Kρ^Γ$. The corresponding conservation laws determine the dependence of the fluid density on the anisotropic KS volume $V=A_2A_3^2$. These source scalings are then inserted into the symmetric part of the covariant teleparallel field equations and used to reconstruct the functional form of $F(T)$ directly from the KS dynamics. Power-law and exponential ansätze generate distinct invariant reconstruction branches. In the power-law sector, the Chaplygin fluid produces mixed constant-plus-power source terms, while the polytropic sector generates density powers controlled by the polytropic index. In the exponential sector, the natural reconstruction variable is the shifted invariant $X=T_0-T$, leading to shifted teleparallel de Sitter branches. The reconstructed models are interpreted as local anisotropic cosmological sectors and, for contracting angular KS scale factors, as local Kantowski--Sachs black-hole-interior reconstruction branches. The analysis is local and branch-dependent; leading-order viability is assessed through \(F_T>0\) and \(F_{TT}>0\), while a complete perturbative stability analysis is left for future work. The reconstruction is entirely driven by nonlinear matter conservation laws, thereby reversing the standard reconstruction strategy in which the gravitational Lagrangian is prescribed a priori.

</details>


### [156] [Gravito-Electromagnetic perturbation of higher derivative corrected Kerr-Newman black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08333)
*Yuanyuan Ji,Zheng-wen Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在缓慢旋转的4阶导数修正Kerr-Newman黑洞背景下，研究了线性引力-电磁扰动，首次推导了轴性和极性扰动扇区的耦合径向主方程，并扩展了渐近迭代方法以处理耦合二阶常微分方程。结果表明，高导数参数c6,c7,c8会导致显著的等谱性破缺，尤其在高电荷情形下更为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高阶导数修正引力理论中黑洞的拟正则模特性，特别是等谱性破缺现象，以检验广义相对论的可能修正。

Method: 采用微扰方法，在角动量、高阶导数参数和电荷的多个小参数展开下推导耦合主方程；扩展渐近迭代方法以求解耦合二阶常微分方程组，计算拟正则频率。

Result: c6,c7,c8参数导致轴性和极性扇区之间出现显著的等谱性破缺，且在高电荷黑洞中该效应更加明显；通过解析拟合方法参数化了拟正则频率。

Conclusion: 高阶导数修正会打破黑洞微扰理论中常见的轴性-极性等谱性，为检验高阶引力理论提供了可观测特征。

Abstract: We study linear gravito-electromagnetic perturbations of a slowly rotating 4-derivative corrected Kerr-Newman (KN) black hole (BH), using a perturbative approach beyond the KN geometry up to first order in angular momentum, first order in the higher-derivative parameters ci, i = 1,2...8, and 14th order in the BH electric charge. We derive the corrected coupled radial master equations for both axial and polar perturbation sectors. We extend the asymptotic iteration method (AIM) to apply to coupled second-order ordinary differential equations (ODEs) and employ it to compute the quasinormal frequencies (QNFs) of the system.Our results show pronounced isospectrality breakings between the two parity sectors, induced by c6,c7 and c8,particularly for high charge BH.Finally, we parameterize the QNFs via a analytical fitting procedure for broader applicability.

</details>


### [157] [Signal-to-Noise Ratio Contours for LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08445)
*Kai Schmitz,Joseph D. Romano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了LISA自相关测量中任意信号强度下的期望最优信噪比（SNR）表达式，纠正了以往仅适用于弱信号极限的SNR公式误差，并发现LISA SNR存在上限SNR_max ≈ sqrt(T_obs(f_max-f_min))（典型任务参数下约10^4），且SNR等高线受LISA应变噪声曲线乘以欧拉数e的上界约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中常用的LISA随机引力波背景信噪比表达式仅在弱信号极限下有效，导致大量已报道的SNR值不准确，需要推导一个适用于任意信号强度的普适SNR表达式。

Method: 首次严格推导了LISA自相关测量在任意信号强度下的期望最优信噪比解析表达式，并基于该广义表达式分析了SNR的理论上限，构建了广义幂律积分（PLI）灵敏度曲线。

Result: 发现LISA SNR存在上限SNR_max = sqrt(T_obs(f_max-f_min))，典型任务参数下约10^4；LISA SNR等高线受到LISA应变噪声曲线乘以欧拉数e的近似上界约束；信号不显著弱于该范围时需使用新表达式计算SNR。

Conclusion: 本研究修正了LISA随机引力波背景灵敏度评估的标准方法，提供了任意信号强度下准确的SNR计算公式，并揭示了以往文献中大量SNR值被高估的问题。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will search for a stochastic gravitational-wave (GW) background at millihertz frequencies, from both astrophysical and cosmological sources, and thereby open a new chapter in GW astronomy. In the literature, LISA's sensitivity to prospective GW background (GWB) signals is often quantified in terms of an expected signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) assuming perfect knowledge of the detector noise. The commonly employed expression for the SNR is, however, valid only in the limit of a weak GWB signal, which renders a large number of SNR values reported in the literature inaccurate. In this paper, we address this issue by deriving for the first time an expression for the expected optimal SNR of a LISA auto-correlation measurement that is valid at arbitrary signal strength. Based on our generalized expression, we conclude that LISA data worth an observing time of T_obs across the frequency band from f_min to f_max will never yield an SNR in excess of SNR_max = sqrt(T_obs(f_max-f_min)), which evaluates to SNR_max <~ 10^4 for typical mission parameters. We illustrate our findings in terms of generalized power-law-integrated (PLI) sensitivity curves at different SNR levels, i.e., LISA SNR contour lines in plots of the GW energy-density power spectrum. In contrast to earlier work on PLI sensitivity curves, we notably find that the LISA SNR contours are bounded from above, approximately by the LISA strain noise curve multiplied by a factor of Euler's number e. For GWB signals not much weaker than this range, the expected SNR for a LISA auto-correlation measurement needs to be evaluated based on our new expression. Our numerical results for the LISA SNR contours are available on Zenodo [https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21275527].

</details>


### [158] [Search for strong lensing of gravitational waves in the binary black hole events from O1-O4a](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08466)
*Ankur Barsode,Koustav N. Maity,Parameswaran Ajith*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文搜索了LIGO、Virgo和KAGRA（LVK）在O1-O4a观测运行期间可能被强引力透镜化的双黑洞信号对，使用了Posterior Overlap 2.0贝叶斯模型选择管道。未发现强透镜化的证据，透镜化概率均低于0.6%，90%置信上限为1.4%。预测O4、IR1和O5运行中做出3σ探测的概率分别约为20%、23%和67%。研究还强调了包含来自多个引力波目录（包括非LVK目录如IAS和OGC）的事件的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LVK观测到的引力波事件中，有一部分可能被前景星系或星系团强透镜化，产生多重信号副本。识别这些透镜化事件对引力波天体物理学和宇宙学具有重要意义，但以往的搜索存在局限性，需要更系统、高效的搜索方法来寻找透镜化候选体。

Method: 使用Posterior Overlap 2.0贝叶斯模型选择管道识别透镜化候选对；包含LVK、IAS和OGC三个目录的事件；通过真实的背景和前景模拟评估管道的鲁棒性和探测效率；定义了新的统计显著性度量标准（个体和总体层面）；计算透镜化概率并进行上限约束。

Result: 所有候选对的透镜化概率均低于0.6%，未发现强透镜化的证据；90%置信水平下透镜化分数上限为1.4%；前九大候选对中有五个来自非LVK目录（IAS和OGC）；预测O4、IR1和O5运行中做出3σ探测的概率分别为~20%、23%和67%。

Conclusion: 当前数据未发现强透镜化事件，但通过包含多个引力波目录的事件（特别是非LVK目录）提高了搜索的全面性。未来观测运行（尤其是O5）具有较高概率实现引力波强透镜化的可靠探测。

Abstract: A small fraction of the gravitational waves (GWs) currently observable by LIGO, Virgo, and KAGRA (LVK) may be strongly lensed by intervening galaxies and galaxy clusters, potentially producing multiple copies of the same signal. We search for lensed pairs of binary black hole signals detected during the O1-O4a observing runs. We include the events identified by the LVK Collaboration, as well as additional events found by external groups (IAS and OGC). Our search is based on Posterior Overlap 2.0, a fast and efficient Bayesian model-selection pipeline to identify lensed candidates. The search is supplemented by realistic background and foreground simulations to characterize the robustness and detection efficiency of the pipeline, as well as the statistical significance of lensed candidates. We define new metrics to assess the statistical significance of lensing both at the individual and population levels. Our work addresses some of the limitations of previous searches. With the probability of lensing $<0.6\%$ for all pairs, we find no evidence for strong lensing in the data and consequently place a $90\%$ upper bound on the lensing fraction of $1.4\%$. With five out of the top nine lensed candidate pairs being from non-LVK catalogs, we also highlight the importance of searching among events reported by multiple GW catalogs. We forecast that the probabilities of making a $3σ$ detection in the fourth (O4), intermediate (IR1), and fifth (O5) observing runs are $\sim 20\%,\, 23\%$, and $67\%$, respectively.

</details>


### [159] [Horizon-redshift transfer in black-hole direct-wave damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08570)
*Wen-Biao Han,Ye Jiang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了黑洞并合产生的直接波包络阻尼，发现其并非直接测量Kerr表面引力κ，而是通过红移拉伸的近地平源与Kerr屏蔽效应转换得到的有限窗口阻尼γ_eff。对GW250114的分析给出γ_eff/κ≈0.6，与QNM扣除残差及双探测器联合分析一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接波包络是否以Kerr表面引力速率衰减尚不明确，需要理论计算以理解其物理机制。

Method: 通过计算复频率自旋-2、ℓ=m=2 Teukolsky响应，结合Kerr屏蔽和源卷积，将近地平源的红移拉伸映射为无穷远观测的有限窗口包络阻尼。

Result: GW250114的直接波包络阻尼γ_eff/κ≈0.6，即γ_eff≈0.4 ms^{-1}；不同源实现（瞬时源、有限持续时间源、试验粒子源、径向归一化源）均给出γ_eff<κ。

Conclusion: 直接波包络阻尼是地平红移转移可观测量，而非表面引力的直接测量。

Abstract: Direct waves from black-hole mergers may probe horizon dynamics, but their observed envelopes need not decay at the Kerr surface-gravity rate. We compute the complex-frequency spin-$-2$, $\ell=m=2$ Teukolsky response driven by a redshift-stretched near-horizon source. Through Kerr screening and source convolution, the calculation maps the local surface-gravity scale $κ$ into the finite-window envelope damping $γ_{\rm eff}$ measured at infinity. For GW250114, this calculation gives $γ_{\rm eff}/κ\simeq0.6$, or $γ_{\rm eff}\simeq0.4~{\rm ms}^{-1}$, consistent with QNM-subtracted residuals and a joint H1--L1 residual analysis. An instantaneous-source control recovers the impulse-response damping near $κ$, whereas finite-duration plunge-source, test-particle and radial-normalized source realizations give $γ_{\rm eff}<κ$. A residual-level check in GW231226 favours the same finite-window damping prediction. These results identify direct-wave envelope damping as a horizon-redshift transfer observable rather than a direct measurement of surface gravity.

</details>


### [160] [Beyond black hole spectroscopy: Quasinormal mode contamination by massless scalars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08619)
*Miguel Yulo Asuncion,Giovanni D'Addario,Thomas P. Sotiriou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了shift-symmetric Horndeski作用量中黑洞微扰对广义相对论环信号的影响，发现标量-高斯-博内耦合是导致频率偏移和污染的主要来源，两种效应出现在同一微扰阶次。若放松对标量振幅的假设，污染可在更低阶出现并主导频率偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑洞环信号检验GR的方法主要关注Kerr度规准正则模频率的偏移，但近年研究表明环信号还会受到超越GR场景中新场准正则模频率的污染。本文旨在系统研究shift-symmetric Horndeski理论中黑洞微扰如何影响环信号，特别是标量场与引力耦合对频率偏移和污染的贡献。

Method: 采用线性微扰理论，对标量场和引力进行线性化处理，同时利用标量电荷与黑洞质量比q进行微扰展开。假设标量振幅受q抑制，在q²阶分析标量-高斯-博内耦合对频率偏移和污染的贡献。随后放松该假设，考察污染在更低阶的出现情况。

Result: 在标量振幅受q抑制的假设下，标量-高斯-博内耦合是唯一对频率偏移和污染都有贡献的项，且两种效应出现在同一微扰阶（q²）。若放松该假设，污染可在q的更低阶出现并主导频率偏移，此时污染还受到额外耦合的次领头阶修正。

Conclusion: 表明在超越GR的理论中，黑洞环信号不仅包含频率偏移，还包含来自新场的污染信号。污染与频率偏移的相对重要性取决于标量振幅的假设，这为未来环信号检验GR提供了新的理论指导和分析框架。

Abstract: Testing General Relativity (GR) with black hole ringdowns has conventionally focused on attempting to detect shifts away from the quasinormal mode (QNM) frequencies of the Kerr metric. It has recently been argued, however, that the ringdown signal will also be contaminated with the QNM frequencies of any new fields that are present in a beyond-GR scenario, provided that they couple nonminimally to gravity. We study black hole perturbations for the shift-symmetric Horndeski action, which includes all interactions between a massless scalar and gravity that lead to second order equations upon variation. We perturb linearly in the field and also employ a perturbative expansion in the scalar charge per unit black hole mass, $q$. Assuming that the scalar amplitude is suppressed by $q$, we demonstrate that, to order $q^2$, the coupling between the scalar and the Gauss-Bonnet invariant is the only term that contributes to both frequency shifts and contamination, and that the two effects appear at the same perturbative order. If the assumption about the suppression of the scalar amplitude is relaxed, contamination can appear at leading order in $q$, and hence dominate over frequency shifts. In this case, contamination also receives subleading corrections from an additional coupling

</details>


### [161] [Shadows and lensing signatures of a rotating black hole in a Hernquist dark matter halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08650)
*A. A. Araújo Filho,Arun Kumar,N. Heidari,C. F. S. Pereira,Amilcar R. Queiroz,V. B. Bezerra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入Hernquist暗物质晕的旋转黑洞的光学性质，通过非复数化Newman-Janis方法构建类Kerr几何，推导了零测地线方程、有效势和光子轨道，计算了阴影轮廓和引力透镜效应，并利用EHT对Sgr A*和M87*的观测以及ESO325-G004的爱因斯坦环对暗晕参数进行了约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究暗物质晕对黑洞光学观测特征（阴影、引力透镜）的影响，并利用实际天文观测数据约束暗晕参数，为探测暗物质提供新的观测途径。

Method: 采用非复数化Newman-Janis变换从静态Hernquist黑洞生成旋转解；通过Hamilton-Jacobi方程分离变量得到临界碰撞参数；利用EHT的Sgr A*和M87*阴影直径数据以及ESO325-G004的爱因斯坦环数据进行参数约束。

Result: 旋转参数主要导致阴影偏移和畸变；Hernquist暗晕增大光子捕获区域和阴影尺寸。Sgr A*给出最强约束：$\hatρ\sim(2.7-3.8)\times10^{-3}$ (1σ) 和 $\hatρ\sim(4.1-5.2)\times10^{-3}$ (2σ)；ESO325-G004给出$0\leq\hatρ\lesssim0.00939$ (1σ) 和 $0\leq\hatρ\lesssim0.01963$ (2σ)。

Conclusion: 暗物质晕显著影响黑洞的光学观测特征，通过对阴影和引力透镜的观测可以有效约束暗晕参数，其中Sgr A*的观测提供了最严格的限制。

Abstract: We investigate the optical properties of a rotating black hole immersed in a Hernquist dark matter halo. The spacetime is generated from a static Hernquist black hole through the noncomplexification version of the Newman-Janis procedure, yielding a Kerr-like geometry whose halo contribution is encoded in the radial function $Δ(r)$ \cite{AraujoFilho:2026hernquist}. We derive the null geodesic equations, effective potentials, radial acceleration, and representative three-dimensional photon trajectories around the event horizon and ergoregion. Using the separability of the Hamilton-Jacobi equation, we obtain the critical impact parameters of unstable spherical photon orbits and construct the shadow contours for a distant observer. The rotation parameter mainly shifts and distorts the shadow, whereas the Hernquist halo enlarges the photon capture region and increases the apparent shadow size. Comparing the area-equivalent shadow diameter with the Event Horizon Telescope measurements of Sgr A$^\ast$ and M87$^\ast$, we constrain the dimensionless halo parameter $\hatρ=M^2ρ$. The strongest restriction comes from Sgr A$^\ast$, giving $\hatρ\sim(2.7-3.8)\times10^{-3}$ at $1σ$ and $\hatρ\sim(4.1-5.2)\times10^{-3}$ at $2σ$. We also analyze strong- and weak-field gravitational lensing. In the strong-field regime, the halo shifts the unstable photon orbit and critical impact parameter, controlling the logarithmic deflection angle and the position of relativistic images. In the weak-field regime, the halo contributes already to the leading bending angle and enhances deviations from Kerr as $ρ$ grows. From the Einstein ring of ESO325-G004, we further obtain $0\leq\hatρ\lesssim0.00939$ at $1σ$ and $0\leq\hatρ\lesssim0.01963$ at $2σ$.

</details>

### [162] [Braiding structures on categorical multi-Interval Jones-Wassermann subfactor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08296)
*Zhengwei Liu,Yuze Ruan*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文为多区间Jones-Wassermann子因子平面代数构造了编织结构，证明了其自对偶性，并展示了这些编织结构诱导了平衡超椭圆映射类群的射影酉表示，最后通过2-box Fourier对偶推导了广义Verlinde公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为多区间Jones-Wassermann子因子平面代数建立编织结构，进而揭示其与酉模融合范畴的高亏格数据之间的深层联系。

Method: 通过构造多区间Jones-Wassermann子因子平面代数上的编织结构，利用该结构证明子因子的自对偶性，并证明编织诱导平衡超椭圆映射类群的射影酉表示。

Result: 成功构造了编织结构；给出了子因子自对偶性的新证明；证明了编织结构诱导平衡超椭圆映射类群的射影酉表示；通过2-box Fourier对偶推导了广义Verlinde公式。

Conclusion: 该构造有效编码了底范畴的非平凡高亏格数据，建立了平面代数与高亏格模空间之间的桥梁。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct braiding structures on the multi-interval Jones-Wassermann subfactor planar algebra associated with any unitary modular fusion category. Utilizing this construction, we provide a new proof of the self-duality of these subfactors. Furthermore, we demonstrate that these braidings induce a projective unitary representation of the balanced superelliptic mapping class group; consequently, these structures effectively encode the non-trivial higher-genus data of the underlying category. As an application of this correspondence, we derive a generalized Verlinde formula as 2-box Fourier duality of the planar algebra.

</details>


### [163] [Proof of Rump's Retraction Conjecture for Quasilinear Cycle Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08609)
*Carsten Dietzel*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文证明了每个非平凡拟线性循环集都是可收缩的，从而解决了Rump关于有限拟线性循环集的收缩猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非退化循环集是Yang-Baxter方程非退化对合解的代数框架，其中阿贝尔群上的循环集（如平移不变和拟线性循环集）在Yang-Baxter方程的收缩问题研究中具有特殊重要性。本文旨在解决Rump提出的关于有限拟线性循环集可收缩性的猜想。

Method: 作者通过分析拟线性循环集的结构性质，证明每个非平凡拟线性循环集都是可收缩的，从而在有限情形下解决了收缩问题。

Result: 成功证明了Rump的猜想：每个非平凡有限拟线性循环集都是可收缩的。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了有限拟线性循环集的收缩问题，为Yang-Baxter方程理论中循环集的结构研究提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Nondegenerate cycle sets were introduced by Rump as an algebraic framework for nondegenerate, involutive solutions to the Yang--Baxter equation. Nondegenerate cycle set structures on abelian groups, such as translation-invariant and quasilinear cycle sets, are of particular interest when studying the retraction problem in the theory of the Yang--Baxter equation. In this article, we solve the retraction problem for finite quasilinear cycle sets by showing that each nontrivial quasilinear cycle set is retractable, thus proving a conjecture of Rump.

</details>

### [164] [Kime-Representation Formulations of Three Open Problems in the Foundations of Classical Mechanics: Uncertainty, Invariant Entropy, and Directional Degrees of Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07851)
*Ivo D. Dinov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在复数时间（kime）表示框架下，对经典力学基础中的三个开放问题（经典熵不确定原理的推广、坐标不变测度与熵的表征、经典相对论自旋1/2类比）给出了自洽的数学表述。通过将kime锥精确辛等同于单自由度相空间的action-angle图，建立了kime测度为Liouville测度、相位律为Liouville密度角度条件化的桥梁。具体证明了：kime柱面上的尖锐熵不确定关系及其极值族、非正则不确定关系（修正项为Poisson括号几何平均）、多自由度聚合界（通过Williamson标准型和Fischer不等式），以及kime相位扩散导致单调熵增长且最终达到等分（Haar均匀）相位律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典力学基础中存在三个长期未解决的开放问题：(I)经典熵不确定原理如何从正则变量推广到非正则变量及多自由度系统；(II)坐标不变测度与熵的存在性条件——为何连续物理量必须配对才能定义不变熵；(III)如何构建经典相对论性的方向自由度（即自旋1/2系统的经典类比）。这些问题分别涉及信息论、几何力学和相对论量子力学的交叉领域，缺乏统一的数学框架。

Method: 采用复数时间（kime）表示法，将kime锥通过精确辛同胚映射到单自由度相空间的action-angle图上。在此框架下，kime测度自然成为Liouville测度，相位律成为Liouville密度的角度条件分布。具体技术工具包括：von Mises分布与高斯分布的极值族分析、Poisson括号的几何平均、Williamson标准型、Fischer不等式以及symplectic Schur-Horn类型问题。

Result: (i)证明了kime柱面上的尖锐熵不确定关系，其极值族为von Mises×高斯分布，同时得到了由von Mises律饱和的尖锐圆形Fisher信息不等式；(ii)证明了精确的非正则不确定关系，修正项为Poisson括号的几何平均，澄清了期望括号的猜想作用；(iii)通过Williamson标准型和Fischer不等式证明了多自由度聚合界，并将每自由度精细化问题归结为辛Schur-Horn类型的精确开放问题；(iv)证明了kime相位扩散导致单调熵增长，且等分（Haar均匀）相位律是熵最大化的平衡分布。

Conclusion: 该工作为经典力学基础中的三个开放问题提供了统一的复数时间（kime）表示框架，建立了熵不确定原理、坐标不变测度和经典自旋类比之间的深层数学联系。kime表示将统计变异性与相空间几何自然结合，不仅解决了经典信息论与力学交叉领域的关键问题，还为未来探索经典-量子对应、相对论统计力学以及自旋系统的经典模拟提供了新的理论工具。文中提出的symplectic Schur-Horn类型问题标志着该方向有待进一步突破的难点。

Abstract: We give mathematically self-contained formulations, in the complex-time (kime) representation, of three open problems from the foundations of classical mechanics: (I) the extension of the classical entropic uncertainty principle to non-canonical variables and to multiple degrees of freedom; (II) the characterization of coordinate-invariant measures and entropies, i.e., the question of why continuous physical quantities must be paired for an invariant entropy to exist; and (III) the construction of a classical relativistic directional degree of freedom (a classical analogue of a spin-1/2 system). Throughout, the kime phase is interpreted {statistically as a latent circular random variable whose law Φmodels the intrinsic trial-to-trial variability of repeated, identically controlled experiments indexed by the kime magnitude. The mathematical bridge is an exact symplectic identification of the kime cone with the action-angle chart of a one-degree-of-freedom phase space, under which the kime measure is the Liouville measure and the phase law becomes the angular conditional of a Liouville density. Specifically, we (i) prove a sharp entropic uncertainty relation on the kime cylinder whose extremal family is von Mises x Gaussian, together with a sharp circular Fisher-information inequality saturated exactly by von Mises laws; (ii) prove an exact non-canonical uncertainty relation in which the correction term is the geometric mean of the Poisson bracket, clarifying the conjectured role of the expected bracket; (iii) prove aggregate multi-degree-of-freedom bounds via the Williamson normal form and Fischer's inequality, and isolate the per-degree-of-freedom refinement as a precise open problem of symplectic Schur-Horn type; (iv) prove that diffusion of the kime phase produces monotone entropy growth with the equipartitioned (Haar-uniform) phase law.

</details>


### [165] [An edge-bicolored graph approach to the Ising model on random regular graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07867)
*Michael Borinsky,Shiyue Ren,Maximilian Wiesmann*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过解析组合方法精确求解了随机正则图系综上的铁磁伊辛模型，通过将配分函数表示为标记边双色图的生成函数，从这些图的渐近计数得到了热力学极限下的自由能，并揭示了一个具有平均场普适类临界指数的二级相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为随机正则图上的伊辛模型提供精确解，填补该领域理论空白。

Method: 使用解析组合学方法，将配分函数表达为标记边双色图的生成函数，通过渐近计数获取热力学极限下的自由能。

Result: 得到了自由能的精确表达式，并观察到二级相变，其临界指数属于平均场普适类。

Conclusion: 随机正则图上的铁磁伊辛模型存在平均场类型的相变行为，且可通过解析组合方法精确求解。

Abstract: We give an exact solution of the ferromagnetic Ising model on a random regular graph ensemble via analytic combinatorics. Expressing the partition function as the generating function of labeled edge-bicolored graphs, we obtain the free energy in the thermodynamic limit from the asymptotic enumeration of these graphs. A simple analysis of the resulting formula reveals a second-order phase transition with critical exponents of the mean-field universality class.

</details>


### [166] [The Periodic Table and the Group SO(4,4): II. Double SO(4,2)-tower](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08105)
*V. V. Varlamov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用李代数$\mathfrak{so}(4,4)$的权图理论对元素周期系进行群论解释：四个量子数$n,l,m,s$对应Cartan生成元的本征值，其根系构成四维自对偶多面体（24-胞），第四Cartan生成元（自旋）的作用导致24-胞投影为两个三维立方八面体（$\mathfrak{so}(4,2)$根系），物理上对应自旋双重性；详细研究了周期系统中能量级结构、门捷列夫与西博格双SO(4,2)-塔及10周期扩展，解释了周期长度2,8,8,18,18,32,32,...的周期加倍现象，并指出反物质自然纳入该群论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为元素周期系提供严格的群论数学基础，统一解释量子数与周期律的结构关系，将自旋双重性、周期加倍现象及反物质纳入同一李代数框架。

Method: 基于李代数$\mathfrak{so}(4,4)$的根系与权图理论，将四个量子数对应Cartan生成元本征值，利用根系自对偶24-胞结构及向$\mathfrak{so}(4,2)$子代数的投影（第四Cartan生成元导致分裂），分析周期系能量级结构。

Result: $\mathfrak{so}(4,4)$根系构成四维24-胞；第四Cartan生成元将Cartan-Weyl基分裂为两个同构于$\mathfrak{so}(4,2)$ Yao基的结构，对应24-胞投影为两个三维立方八面体（自旋双重性）；解释了周期长度序列2,8,8,18,18,32,32,...的加倍机制；反物质自然纳入该群论方案。

Conclusion: $\mathfrak{so}(4,4)$李代数的权图理论为元素周期系提供了统一且自洽的群论描述，自旋、周期加倍及反物质均在该框架内得到自然解释。

Abstract: A group-theoretic interpretation of the periodic system of elements is given within the framework of the weight diagram of the Lie algebra $\mathfrak{so}(4,4)$ of the fourth rank, where the four quantum numbers $n$, $l$, $m$, $s$ correspond to the eigenvalues (weights) of the Cartan generators of the maximal Abelian subalgebra (the maximal torus of the group SO(4,4)). It is shown that the root system of the algebra $\mathfrak{so}(4,4)$ forms a regular four-dimensional self-dual polyhedron (24-cell). The action of the fourth Cartan generator associated with spin leads to a splitting of the Cartan-Weyl basis of the algebra $\mathfrak{so}(4,4)$ into two structurally identical bases, each of which is isomorphic to the Yao basis of the subalgebra $\mathfrak{so}(4,2)$ (the Lie algebra of the conformal group). At this point, a four-dimensional 24-cell is projected onto two three-dimensional cuboctahedra, each of which defines the root system of the subalgebra $\mathfrak{so}(4,2)$. This splitting physically corresponds to spin doubling (two-valuedness). The structure of the energy levels of a periodic system is studied, the states of which (chemical elements) are represented as nodes of the weight diagram of the group algebra $\mathfrak{so}(4,4)$. The structure of the double SO(4,2)-towers of Mendeleev, Seaborg, and 10-periodic extension is examined in detail. The period doubling associated with the sequence of period lengths 2, 8, 8, 18, 18, 32, 32, $\ldots$ of the periodic system of elements is explained by the action of the fourth Cartan generator. It is shown that antimatter (Mendeleev anti-table consisting of antihydrogen, antihelium, antilitium, $\ldots$) is naturally included in the general group-theoretic scheme of description of the periodic table.

</details>


### [167] [Wigner symmetries single out symmetric Wasserstein distances in all finite dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08298)
*Gergely Bunth*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子Wasserstein距离，证明了正半定代价算子由纯态距离唯一确定；证明了Wasserstein等距幺半群恰好由Wigner对称性（酉共轭和反酉共轭）构成当且仅当距离在纯态上酉不变；建立了二次代价算子与Hilbert-Schmidt框架型算子之间的显式互逆映射，揭示了各向同性代价至少需要d²-1个自伴生成元，且取等时其无迹部分构成Hilbert-Schmidt标准正交基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立量子Wasserstein距离的对称性理论，阐明代价算子、距离几何、表示论、算子理论和框架理论中对称性概念的等价性，刻画Wasserstein等距变换群。

Method: 利用纯态距离确定代价算子；通过表示论分析Wigner对称性；构造代价算子与Hilbert-Schmidt框架型算子之间的互逆映射；利用紧框架性质刻画各向同性代价。

Result: 正半定代价算子由纯态距离完全确定；Wasserstein等距幺半群为Wigner对称性当且仅当距离在纯态上酉不变；各向同性代价需至少d²-1个自伴生成元，取等时无迹部分构成Hilbert-Schmidt标准正交基。

Conclusion: 几何、表示论、算子理论和框架理论中的对称性概念都确定了同一单参数族的量子Wasserstein距离。

Abstract: We study the quantum Wasserstein distances introduced by De Palma and Trevisan associated with quadratic cost operators generated by families of self-adjoint observables. We first show that an arbitrary positive semidefinite cost operator is completely determined by the restriction of the corresponding Wasserstein distance to pairs of pure states. This allows geometric invariance of the pure-state distance to be translated directly into invariance of the cost operator.
  Within the class of nonzero quadratic costs generated by at most $d^2-1$ observables on a $d$-dimensional Hilbert space, we prove that the Wasserstein isometry monoid consists exactly of the Wigner symmetries, that is, unitary and antiunitary conjugations, if and only if the distance is invariant under unitary conjugations on pure states. Equivalently, the cost operator intertwines the adjoint representation of the unitary group and is a positive scalar multiple of the identity on the traceless subspace.
  We further construct explicit mutually inverse maps between quadratic cost operators generated by observables and Hilbert--Schmidt frame-type operators formed from their traceless parts. Under this correspondence, isotropy of the cost is equivalent to the tight frame property of the associated Hilbert--Schmidt operator. Consequently, a nonzero isotropic cost requires at least $d^2-1$ self-adjoint generators, and equality holds precisely when their traceless parts form, up to a common scale, a Hilbert--Schmidt orthonormal basis. Thus the geometric, representation-theoretic, operator-theoretic, and frame-theoretic notions of symmetry all determine the same one-parameter family of quantum Wasserstein distances.

</details>


### [168] [Renormalization flows for 1D mixed states and a quantum Goursat lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08568)
*Léo Le-Nestour,David Pérez-García,Alberto Ruiz-de-Alarcón*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维拓扑有序模型边界态的重整化流，利用C*-Hopf代数上的完全正映射描述边界态在噪声干扰和粗粒化下的演化，发现收敛轨迹对应于有限*-量子超群，并提出了量子Goursat引理来分类所有收敛重整化轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将重整化框架应用于非手征二维拓扑有序模型的边界态，理解微观模型到宏观有效描述的关系，并揭示有限*-量子超群的物理意义。

Method: 以有限维C*-Hopf代数的表示为初始重整化不动点，引入均匀on-site噪声量子通道扰动，重复进行粗粒化操作。将流过程转化为C*-Hopf代数上的完全正映射（或等价地，其包络代数上的正线性泛函）的迭代，受卷积幂控制。通过有限*-量子超群刻画收敛的矩阵积密度算子不动点，并利用Goursat引理（及其量子推广）进行分类。

Result: 建立了边界态重整化流的代数描述框架，证明了收敛轨迹对应有限*-量子超群；对有限群代数及其对偶给出了基于Goursat引理的显式分类；证明了有限维C*-Hopf代数的量子Goursat引理，为所有收敛重整化轨迹提供了结构性描述。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限*-量子超群提供了具体的物理实现，并发展了独立的量子Goursat引理，为理解拓扑有序系统边界态的普适行为提供了代数工具。

Abstract: Renormalization provides a framework for relating microscopic models of physical systems to effective descriptions at larger length scales. This procedure is studied for the boundary states of non-chiral two-dimensional topologically ordered models. The initial data consist of renormalization fixed points built from representations of finite-dimensional $C^*$-Hopf algebras, which are then perturbed by uniform on-site noise quantum channels and repeatedly coarse-grained. The resulting flows admit an intrinsic algebraic description in terms of completely positive maps on the $C^*$-Hopf algebra or, equivalently, positive linear functionals on its enveloping $C^*$-Hopf algebra. Their iteration is governed by convolution powers, and convergent trajectories yield new matrix product density operator fixed points, described by finite $*$-quantum hypergroups. This provides a concrete physical interpretation of such structures. For finite group algebras and their duals, we provide explicit classifications via Goursat's lemma for groups. Finally, we formulate and prove a quantum generalization of Goursat's lemma for finite-dimensional $C^*$-Hopf algebras, a result of independent interest, which gives an explicit structural description of all convergent renormalization trajectories.

</details>


### [169] [Approximate eigenfunctions for some aperiodic crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08320)
*Long Meng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对非周期晶体中的哈密顿量 $H_\varepsilon = T(-i\nabla_x + \mathbf{A}(x,\varepsilon x)) + V(x,\varepsilon x)$，假设系数在快变量 $x$ 上关于晶格 $\mathbb{L}$ 周期，慢变量 $X=\varepsilon x$ 作为参数。通过分析依赖于 $(k,X)$ 的周期族 $h(k,X)$，在特定能量 $e_0$ 和点 $(k_0,X_0)$ 处满足适当假设下，构造了局部化的近似本征函数 $\Phi_\varepsilon$，使得误差阶达到 $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{\frac{m}{2}+\frac{1}{4}})$，其中 $m\in\{1,2\}$，且 $\Phi_\varepsilon$ 的 $L^2$ 范数近似为单胞体积倒数的平方根。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非周期晶体（如准晶、非公度体系）的有效模型缺乏周期性，经典Bloch定理不再适用。构建这类系统的近似本征态（特别是局域化态）是数学物理中的重要问题。

Method: 引入快变量 $x$ 和慢变量 $X=\varepsilon x$，将问题转化为含参数 $(k,X)$ 的周期算子族 $h(k,X)$ 在 $L^2_{\rm per}$ 上的分析。利用关于 $(k_0,X_0)$ 的局部假设，通过多尺度渐近展开构造近似本征函数。

Result: 对充分小的 $\varepsilon$，存在 $m\in\{1,2\}$ 和 $\mu\in\mathbb{R}$，使得构造的 $\Phi_\varepsilon$ 满足 $\|(H_\varepsilon-e_0-\varepsilon^{m/2}\mu)\Phi_\varepsilon\| = \mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{m/2+1/4})$，且 $\|\Phi_\varepsilon\| = |\mathbb{R}^d/\mathbb{L}|^{-1/2} + \mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\varepsilon})$。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了从周期算子族 $h(k,X)$ 的局部性质到非周期晶体 $H_\varepsilon$ 近似本征态的一般性构造框架，误差阶明确，适用于 Dirac 和 Schrödinger 两类算子。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider Hamiltonians for aperiodic crystals of the form \begin{align*}
  H_\varepsilon:=T(-i\nabla_x+{\mathbf A}(x,\varepsilon x))+V(x,\varepsilon x),\qquad x\in {\mathbb R}^d \end{align*} where $T$ represents either a Dirac operators or a Schrödinger operator, and $x\mapsto {\mathbf A}(x,X)$ and $x\mapsto V(x,X)$ are $\mathbb L$-periodic with respect to some lattice $\mathbb L\subset{\mathbb R}^d$.
  Let \begin{align*}
  (k,X)\ni {\mathbb R}^d\times {\mathbb R}^d\mapsto h(k,X):=T(-i\nabla_x+k+{\mathbf A}(x,X))+V(x,X) \end{align*} be a family of operators acting on $L^2_{\rm per}(\mathbb{R}^d/\mathbb{L})$ with periodic boundary conditions. We show that, under some suitable assumptions on the family of operators $ (h(k,X))_{k,X}$ around an energy level $e_0\in {\mathbb R}$ and some points $(k_0,X_0)\in {\mathbb R}^d\times {\mathbb R}^d$, one can construct localized approximate eigenfunctions $Φ_\varepsilon\in L^2({\mathbb R}^d)$ of the operator $H_\varepsilon$ such that for $\varepsilon$ small enough and for some $m\in \{1,2\}$ and $μ\in {\mathbb R}$, \begin{align}\label{eq:abstract}
  \|(H_\varepsilon-e_0-\varepsilon^{\frac{m}{2}}μ)Φ_\varepsilon\|_{L^2({\mathbb R}^d)}={\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{\frac{m}{2}+\frac{1}{4}}). \end{align} with \begin{align*}
  \|Φ_\varepsilon\|_{L^2({\mathbb R}^d)}=\frac{1}{|{\mathbb R}^d/\mathbb L|^{1/2}}+{\mathcal O}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}). \end{align*}

</details>


### [170] [The Fock Space Dynamics of Causal Fermion Systems: Non-Abelian Gauge Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08488)
*Claudio Dappiaggi,Felix Finster,Niky Kamran,Moritz Reintjes*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了因果费米系统在闵可夫斯基空间中的极限情况，表明其因果作用原理能够产生包含非阿贝尔规范场和狄拉克场的微扰量子场论的线性福克空间动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图建立因果费米系统（一种量子时空的数学模型）与标准微扰量子场论之间的严格数学联系，从而为从底层时空理论推导出已知量子场论提供理论基础。

Method: 通过研究因果费米系统在闵可夫斯基空间中的一个极限情况（limiting case），分析因果作用原理如何退化为描述自由量子场论（包括非阿贝尔规范场和狄拉克场）的线性福克空间动力学。

Result: 在该极限下，因果作用原理可以还原出微扰量子场论中处理非阿贝尔规范场和狄拉克场所用的标准线性福克空间结构及动力学方程。

Conclusion: 因果费米系统框架在适当的极限下能够统一描述非阿贝尔规范场和费米子场的量子行为，为量子引力与量子场论的统一提供了新的数学途径。

Abstract: A limiting case is worked out in which the causal action principle for causal fermion systems describing Minkowski space gives rise to the linear Fock space dynamics of perturbative quantum field theory including non-abelian gauge fields and Dirac fields.

</details>


### [171] [Multifractality of Semiclassical Measures on Star Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08496)
*Marius Nietschmann*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子星图在大边数极限下的本征函数，证明其边质量分布沿特定本征值子序列可实现所有允许的多重分形标度律，并针对准等边星图给出构造性结果，表明量子星图是介于局域化和均匀分布之间的完整多重分形行为的显式模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子星图在大边数极限下本征函数的边质量分布能否实现从局域化到均匀分布之间所有允许的多重分形标度行为，并给出显式构造。

Method: 利用半经典测度关联的边质量分布，分析本征函数在大边数极限下的标度行为。对一般边长情形沿特定本征值子序列证明标度律可实现性；对拟等边星图从预设概率测度出发构造图结构并定位谱簇内的本征值。

Result: 证明了对于一般边长的量子星图，沿适当本征值子序列，边质量分布可实现所有允许的多重分形标度律；对于准等边星图，可从任意预设概率测度构造出图并找到谱簇内本征值，使其本征函数复现相同标度行为。

Conclusion: 量子星图是一个能够显式实现从局域化到均匀分布之间完整多重分形行为的模型，展示了其本征函数在标度行为上的丰富性。

Abstract: We study eigenfunctions of quantum star graphs in the large edge number limit through the edge-mass distributions associated with their semiclassical measures. For generic edge lengths, we show that these distributions can realize every admissible multifractal scaling law along suitable subsequences of eigenvalues. We also prove a constructive result for quasi-equilateral star graphs. Starting from prescribed probability measures, we construct graphs and locate eigenvalues inside spectral clusters whose eigenfunctions reproduce the same scaling behavior. These results show that quantum star graphs form an explicit model realizing the full range of admissible multifractal behavior between localization and equidistribution.

</details>


### [172] [Quantum filtering and propagation of chaos for open quantum systems, with applications to quantum feedback control and quantum mean-field games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08507)
*Vassili N. Kolokoltsov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了量子滤波方程的数学理论，包括从基本原理的严格推导、大数定律极限（混沌传播）及相关主题，并讨论了在反馈控制、量子动力学和平均场博弈中的应用。该理论由V.P. Belavkin约40年前提出，作者近期解决了无穷维量子系统中混合态滤波方程的严格数学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子滤波理论自Belavkin提出后吸引了大量研究者，但无穷维量子系统中混合态滤波方程的严格数学理论长期悬而未决，作者近期解决了该问题，因此撰写综述系统呈现完整数学框架。

Method: 基于基本公理对量子滤波方程进行严格的数学推导，建立无穷维量子系统混合态的滤波方程理论，并推导相应的大数定律极限（混沌传播）。

Result: 成功建立了无穷维量子系统混合态量子滤波方程的严格数学理论，实现了从基本原理到滤波方程的完整演绎。

Conclusion: 量子滤波方程的严格数学理论已完整建立，可应用于反馈控制、量子动力学和平均场博弈等领域。

Abstract: The theory of quantum filtering (of quantum continuous measurements) was developed by V.P. Belavkin about 40 years ago. Since then it attracted attention of numerous investigators including mathematicians, theoretical and experimental physicists. However, the rigorous mathematical theory of the filtering equations for mixed states in basic infinite-dimensional quantum systems remained an open problem, which was resolved by the author recently. This survey paper presents in full the mathematical theory of quantum filtering equations, their rigorous derivation from basic principles, the corresponding law of large number limits (propagation of chaos) and related topics. Applications to feedback control, quantum dynamic and mean-field games are discussed.

</details>

### [173] [On the Self-adjoint properties of the standard Whittaker $(\mathfrak{g}, K)$-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08245)
*Kenji Taniguchi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了实分裂约化线性李群情形下标准Whittaker $(\mathfrak{g}, K)$-模的结构。该模是具有固定无穷小特征的Harish-Chandra $(\mathfrak{g}, K)$-模范畴中的内射对象。确定了该模的全局特征，主要定理证明了其具有自对偶结构，并给出了秩二分裂群$SL(3,\mathbb{R})$, $Sp(2,\mathbb{R})$和$G_{2}$(split)的显式socle滤过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解标准Whittaker模的结构，特别是其在Harish-Chandra模范畴中的内射性、全局特征以及自对偶性质，并给出低秩分裂群的显式socle滤过。

Method: 采用表示论和Harish-Chandra模的理论框架，通过分析无穷小特征和socle滤过来研究标准Whittaker模的结构性质。

Result: 证明了标准Whittaker模具有自对偶结构，确定了其全局特征，并给出了$SL(3,\mathbb{R})$, $Sp(2,\mathbb{R})$和$G_{2}$(split)的显式socle滤过。

Conclusion: 标准Whittaker模在研考的群类中具有内射性和自对偶性，其结构可通过socle滤过完全描述。

Abstract: The structure of the standard Whittaker $(\mathfrak{g}, K)$-module is examined in the case when the group in question is a real split reductive linear Lie group. This module is an injective object in the category of Harish-Chandra $(\mathfrak{g}, K)$-modules which admit a fixed infinitesimal character. The global character of this module is determined. The main theorem of this paper is that it has a self-adjoint structure. Also obtained are the explicit socle filtrations of the standard Whittaker $(\mathfrak{g}, K)$-modules for the rank two split groups $SL(3,\mathbb{R})$, $Sp(2,\mathbb{R})$ and $G_{2}$(split).

</details>


### [174] [Representations of $p$-adic reductive groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08308)
*David Renard*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本书系统介绍了p-进约化群的复杂表示理论，从基础概念出发，最终涵盖Bernstein中心理论和Langlands不可约光滑表示分类，共七章及附录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究生及以上层次读者提供p-进约化群表示理论的系统化教材，填补从基础知识到前沿理论（Bernstein中心、Langlands分类）之间的空白。

Method: 采用逐步递进的结构：前四章建立一般框架（含幂等元的代数、全不连通局部紧空间与群、光滑表示及特殊表示类），第五章回顾p-进约化群结构，第六、七章为核心章节，分别阐述Bernstein分解定理与中心刻画、平方可积与缓表示理论及Langlands分类定理的证明。

Result: 成功建立了从基础到前沿的完整体系，证明了Bernstein分解定理、中心描述以及Langlands分类定理等关键结果。

Conclusion: 该书为p-进约化群光滑表示范畴提供了系统且自洽的理论框架，涵盖了Bernstein中心和Langlands分类等核心内容，是相关领域研究和教学的重要参考。

Abstract: This book presents a part of the (complex) representation theory of $p$-adic reductive groups. Starting from a basis accessible to graduate students, it culminates with the theory of the "Bernstein center" and the Langlands classification of irreducible smooth representations.
  \vspace{0.5cm}
  \noindent The book consists of seven chapters, Chapters VI and VII constituting the core of the book. Chapter VI is devoted to the study of the category of smooth representations of a $p$-adic reductive group, establishing among other things the Bernstein decomposition theorem and the description of its center. Chapter VII deals with square-integrable and tempered representations, and the Langlands classification theorem is proved there.
  \vspace{0.5cm}
  \noindent The first four chapters are placed in a more general framework and tackle, in order, the study of algebras with idempotents, totally disconnected locally compact spaces and groups, smooth representations of the latter, and particular classes of representations (compact, unitary, square-integrable). Chapter V is a review of the structure results of $p$-adic reductive groups. Appendices give the elements of category theory and some results in algebra used in the text.

</details>


### [175] [The fundamental theorems of invariant theory for linearly oligomorphic groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08485)
*Alessandro Danelon,Andrew Snowden*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为Harman等人引入的新型无限维代数群建立了不变性理论的第一和第二基本定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广经典群的不变理论至新引入的无限维代数群，填补该领域理论空白。

Method: 基于Harman与第二作者近期引入的新型无限维代数群，建立其不变性理论的第一和第二基本定理。

Result: 成功为这些新型无限维代数群建立了不变性理论的第一和第二基本定理。

Conclusion: 本文完成了对新引入的无限维代数群的不变理论的基本定理证明，拓展了经典不变性理论的适用范围。

Abstract: In recent work, Harman and the second author introduced some new infinite dimensional algebraic groups that generalize the classical groups. In this paper, we establish versions of the first and second fundamental theorems of invariant theory for them.

</details>
